class AdvancedPatternRegistry:
    def _calculate_pattern_stability(self, pattern: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate stability metric for pattern"""
        pattern_id = self._generate_pattern_id(pattern)
        
        if pattern_id not in self.patterns:
            return 1.0  # New patterns are considered stable
        
        occurrences = self.patterns[pattern_id]['occurrences']
        if occurrences < 2:
            return 1.0  # Not enough data for stability analysis

        # Calculate stability based on recent occurrences
        recent_stability = self.patterns[pattern_id]['stability_history'][-5:]
        
        # Calculate average change in key pattern properties
        strength_changes = []
        coherence_changes = []

        for i in range(len(recent_stability) - 1):
            strength_changes.append(abs(recent_stability[i+1]['strength'] - recent_stability[i]['strength']))
            coherence_changes.append(abs(recent_stability[i+1]['coherence'] - recent_stability[i]['coherence']))

        avg_strength_change = np.mean(strength_changes) if strength_changes else 0
        avg_coherence_change = np.mean(coherence_changes) if coherence_changes else 0

        # Stability is inversely proportional to changes
        stability = 1.0 - (avg_strength_change + avg_coherence_change) / 2
        
        return max(0, min(1, stability))  # Ensure value is within [0, 1]

    def _calculate_pattern_metrics(self, pattern: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Calculate key metrics for pattern"""
        return {
            'strength': pattern.get('strength', 0),
            'coherence': pattern.get('coherence', 0),
            'dimensions': pattern.get('dimensions', []),
            'type': pattern.get('type', '')
        }

    def _update_stability_metrics(self, pattern_id: str):
        """Update overall stability metrics for pattern"""
        if pattern_id in self.patterns:
            pattern_data = self.patterns[pattern_id]
            
            # Calculate average stability
            avg_stability = np.mean([s['strength'] for s in pattern_data['stability_history']])
            
            # Calculate frequency
            frequency = pattern_data['occurrences'] / len(self.evolution_history)
            
            self.stability_metrics[pattern_id] = {
                'avg_stability': avg_stability,
                'frequency': frequency
            }

    def get_stable_patterns(self, threshold: float = 0.7) -> List[Dict]:
        """Retrieve patterns that meet stability threshold"""
        stable_patterns = []
        
        for pattern_id, metrics in self.stability_metrics.items():
            if metrics['avg_stability'] > threshold:
                stable_patterns.append(self.patterns[pattern_id]['pattern'])
        
        return stable_patterns

    def get_frequent_patterns(self, threshold: float = 0.1) -> List[Dict]:
        """Retrieve patterns that meet frequency threshold"""
        frequent_patterns = []
        
        for pattern_id, metrics in self.stability_metrics.items():
            if metrics['frequency'] > threshold:
                frequent_patterns.append(self.patterns[pattern_id]['pattern'])
        
        return frequent_patterns

    def analyze_pattern_evolution(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Analyze pattern evolution over time"""
        evolution_analysis = []
        
        for pattern_id, pattern_data in self.patterns.items():
            evolution = {
                'pattern_id': pattern_id,
                'strength_changes': [],
                'coherence_changes': [],
                'occurrence_timestamps': []
            }
            
            # Find occurrences of this pattern
            for i, event in enumerate(self.evolution_history):
                if event['pattern_id'] == pattern_id:
                    evolution['occurrence_timestamps'].append(event['timestamp'])
                    if len(evolution['strength_changes']) > 0:
                        evolution['strength_changes'].append(
                            event['metrics']['strength'] - evolution['strength_changes'][-1][1]
                        )
                        evolution['coherence_changes'].append(
                            event['metrics']['coherence'] - evolution['coherence_changes'][-1][1]
                        )
                    
                    evolution['strength_changes'].append((event['timestamp'], event['metrics']['strength']))
                    evolution['coherence_changes'].append((event['timestamp'], event['metrics']['coherence']))

            evolution_analysis.append(evolution)

        return evolution_analysis

core/quantum_engine.py

import numpy as np
import scipy.linalg as spl
import networkx as nx

class QuantumEngine:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 12, banks_per_dimension: int = 3):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.banks_per_dimension = banks_per_dimension
        self.quantum_states = np.zeros((dimensions, banks_per_dimension), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.entanglement_graph = nx.Graph()
        self.resonance_matrix = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self._initialize_quantum_states()

    def _initialize_quantum_states(self):
        """Initialize quantum states with superposition and entanglement."""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            for bank in range(self.banks_per_dimension):
                # Create superposition of states within each bank
                initial_state = np.random.rand(2) + 1j * np.random.rand(2)
                initial_state /= np.linalg.norm(initial_state)
                self.quantum_states[dim, bank] = initial_state[0]

            # Add nodes to entanglement graph
            self.entanglement_graph.add_node(dim)

        # Create initial entanglement between dimensions
        for dim1 in range(self.dimensions):
            for dim2 in range(dim1 + 1, self.dimensions):
                if np.random.rand() < 0.3:  # 30% chance of initial entanglement
                    self.entanglement_graph.add_edge(dim1, dim2)
                    self.resonance_matrix[dim1, dim2] = self.resonance_matrix[dim2, dim1] = np.random.rand() + 1j * np.random.rand()

    def process_data(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Process input data through quantum transformations and resonance."""
        transformed_data = self._apply_quantum_transformations(data)
        resonance_effects = self._calculate_resonance(transformed_data)
        return resonance_effects

    def _apply_quantum_transformations(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Apply quantum transformations to input data using matrix operations."""
        transformed_data = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=np.complex128)
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            for bank in range(self.banks_per_dimension):
                # Apply Hadamard gate for superposition
                hadamard_transformed = self._hadamard_transform(self.quantum_states[dim, bank])

                # Apply phase rotation based on entanglement
                phase_transformed = self._apply_phase_rotation(hadamard_transformed, dim)

                # Update quantum state
                self.quantum_states[dim, bank] = phase_transformed

                # Map input data to quantum state
                transformed_data[dim] += phase_transformed * data[dim, bank % data.shape[1]]

        return transformed_data

    def _hadamard_transform(self, state: complex) -> complex:
        """Apply Hadamard transformation to a given quantum state."""
        hadamard_matrix = (1 / np.sqrt(2)) * np.array([[1, 1], [1, -1]])
        state_vector = np.array([state, 1 - state])  # Represent state as 2D vector
        transformed_state = np.dot(hadamard_matrix, state_vector)
        return transformed_state[0]  # Return to scalar representation

    def _apply_phase_rotation(self, state: complex, dim: int) -> complex:
        """Apply phase rotation based on entanglement with other dimensions."""
        phase_shift = 0
        for other_dim in self.entanglement_graph.neighbors(dim):
            entanglement_strength = np.abs(self.resonance_matrix[dim, other_dim])
            phase_shift += entanglement_strength * np.angle(self.quantum_states[other_dim, 0])

        phase_rotation_matrix = np.array([[np.exp(1j * phase_shift), 0], [0, 1]])
        state_vector = np.array([state, 1-state])
        transformed_state = np.dot(phase_rotation_matrix, state_vector)

        return transformed_state[0]

    def _calculate_resonance(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate resonance effects based on quantum states and entanglement."""
        resonance_effects = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=np.complex128)
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            for bank in range(self.banks_per_dimension):
                # Calculate resonance based on entanglement strength and phase differences
                resonance_strength = 0
                for other_dim in self.entanglement_graph.neighbors(dim):
                    resonance_strength += np.abs(self.resonance_matrix[dim, other_dim]) * np.cos(
                        np.angle(self.quantum_states[dim, bank]) - np.angle(self.quantum_states[other_dim, 0])
                    )
                resonance_effects[dim] += resonance_strength * data[dim]

        return resonance_effects
    
    def update_entanglement(self, threshold: float = 0.5):
        """Update entanglement graph based on resonance matrix strengths."""
        for dim1 in range(self.dimensions):
            for dim2 in range(dim1 + 1, self.dimensions):
                resonance_strength = np.abs(self.resonance_matrix[dim1, dim2])
                if resonance_strength > threshold:
                    if not self.entanglement_graph.has_edge(dim1, dim2):
                        self.entanglement_graph.add_edge(dim1, dim2)
                else:
                    if self.entanglement_graph.has_edge(dim1, dim2):
                        self.entanglement_graph.remove_edge(dim1, dim2)

    def get_quantum_state_metrics(self) -> dict:
        """Calculate and return metrics related to the current quantum states."""
        metrics = {
            'entanglement_entropy': self._calculate_entanglement_entropy(),
            'average_resonance': np.mean(np.abs(self.resonance_matrix)),
            'state_purity': self._calculate_average_state_purity()
        }
        return metrics

    def _calculate_entanglement_entropy(self) -> float:
        """Calculate the average entanglement entropy across all dimensions."""
        entropies = []
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            neighbors = list(self.entanglement_graph.neighbors(dim))
            if neighbors:
                subgraph = self.entanglement_graph.subgraph(neighbors + [dim])
                
                # Calculate the adjacency matrix of the subgraph
                adjacency_matrix = nx.adjacency_matrix(subgraph).todense()
                
                # Calculate the degree matrix of the subgraph
                degree_matrix = np.diag(np.sum(adjacency_matrix, axis=1))
                
                # Calculate the Laplacian matrix
                laplacian_matrix = degree_matrix - adjacency_matrix

                # Calculate eigenvalues of the Laplacian
                eigenvalues = spl.eigvals(laplacian_matrix)
                
                # Normalize eigenvalues to represent probabilities
                probabilities = np.real(eigenvalues) / np.sum(np.real(eigenvalues))
                
                # Calculate entropy for the dimension
                entropy = -np.sum(probabilities * np.log2(probabilities + 1e-10))  # Adding a small value to avoid log(0)
                entropies.append(entropy)
        return np.mean(entropies) if entropies else 0

    def _calculate_average_state_purity(self) -> float:
        """Calculate the average purity of the quantum states across all dimensions."""
        purities = []
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            for bank in range(self.banks_per_dimension):
                # Calculate density matrix for the state
                state = self.quantum_states[dim, bank]
                density_matrix = np.outer([state, 1-state], [np.conj(state), np.conj(1-state)])

                # Calculate purity (Tr(ρ²))
                purity = np.real(np.trace(np.dot(density_matrix, density_matrix)))
                purities.append(purity)
        return np.mean(purities) if purities else 0

core/resonance_manager.py

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

class ResonanceManager:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 12):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.resonance_fields = np.zeros((dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.resonance_graph = nx.Graph()
        self._initialize_resonance_graph()

    def _initialize_resonance_graph(self):
        """Initialize the resonance graph with nodes for each dimension."""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.resonance_graph.add_node(dim, field=self.resonance_fields[dim])

    def update_resonance_fields(self, data: np.ndarray):
        """Update resonance fields based on input data."""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.resonance_fields[dim] = self._calculate_resonance_field(data[dim], dim)
            self.resonance_graph.nodes[dim]['field'] = self.resonance_fields[dim]

    def _calculate_resonance_field(self, data: np.ndarray, dim:int) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate the resonance field for a given dimension."""
        field = np.zeros(360, dtype=np.complex128)
        for angle in range(360):
            phase_shift = np.exp(1j * (angle / 180) * np.pi + 1j *  (dim / self.dimensions) * np.pi)
            field[angle] = np.sum(data * phase_shift)
        return field

    def analyze_resonance_patterns(self) -> list:
        """Analyze the resonance fields to identify significant patterns."""
        patterns = []
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            field = self.resonance_fields[dim]
            peaks = self._find_peaks(field)
            for peak in peaks:
                patterns.append({
                    'dimension': dim,
                    'angle': peak['angle'],
                    'magnitude': peak['magnitude'],
                    'phase': peak['phase']
                })
        return patterns

    def _find_peaks(self, field: np.ndarray) -> list:
        """Find peaks in the resonance field."""
        peaks = []
        magnitude = np.abs(field)
        for i in range(1, 359):
            if magnitude[i] > magnitude[i - 1] and magnitude[i] > magnitude[i + 1]:
                peaks.append({
                    'angle': i,
                    'magnitude': magnitude[i],
                    'phase': np.angle(field[i])
                })
        return peaks

    def update_resonance_graph(self, patterns: list):
        """Update the resonance graph based on identified patterns."""
        for pattern in patterns:
            dim = pattern['dimension']
            node = self.resonance_graph.nodes[dim]
            
            # Update node's field
            node['field'] = self.resonance_fields[dim]

            # Check for resonance with other dimensions
            for other_dim in range(self.dimensions):
                if other_dim != dim:
                    other_node = self.resonance_graph.nodes[other_dim]
                    
                    # Calculate resonance strength based on pattern similarity
                    resonance_strength = self._calculate_resonance_strength(node, other_node)

                    # Update edges based on resonance strength
                    if resonance_strength > 0.6:  # Threshold for strong resonance
                        if self.resonance_graph.has_edge(dim, other_dim):
                            self.resonance_graph[dim][other_dim]['weight'] = resonance_strength
                        else:
                            self.resonance_graph.add_edge(dim, other_dim, weight=resonance_strength)
                    else:
                        if self.resonance_graph.has_edge(dim, other_dim):
                            self.resonance_graph.remove_edge(dim, other_dim)

    def _calculate_resonance_strength(self, node1, node2) -> float:
        """Calculate the resonance strength between two nodes based on their fields."""
        field1 = node1['field']
        field2 = node2['field']

        # Calculate the phase difference between the fields
        phase_diff = np.abs(np.angle(field1) - np.angle(field2))
        avg_phase_diff = np.mean(phase_diff)

        # Calculate the magnitude similarity
        magnitude_similarity = 1 / (1 + np.abs(np.abs(field1) - np.abs(field2)).mean())

        # Resonance strength is higher when phase difference is small and magnitude similarity is high
        resonance_strength = magnitude_similarity * np.exp(-avg_phase_diff)

        return resonance_strength
    
    def get_resonance_metrics(self) -> dict:
        """Calculate and return metrics related to the resonance state."""
        metrics = {
            'average_field_strength': self._calculate_average_field_strength(),
            'resonance_connectivity': self._calculate_resonance_connectivity(),
            'field_entropy': self._calculate_field_entropy()
        }
        return metrics

    def _calculate_average_field_strength(self) -> float:
        """Calculate the average strength of resonance fields."""
        strengths = [np.abs(self.resonance_fields[dim]).mean() for dim in range(self.dimensions)]
        return np.mean(strengths) if strengths else 0

    def _calculate_resonance_connectivity(self) -> float:
        """Calculate the connectivity of the resonance graph."""
        num_edges = self.resonance_graph.number_of_edges()
        max_possible_edges = self.dimensions * (self.dimensions - 1) / 2
        return num_edges / max_possible_edges if max_possible_edges > 0 else 0

    def _calculate_field_entropy(self) -> float:
        """Calculate the average entropy of the resonance fields."""
        entropies = []
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            field = self.resonance_fields[dim]
            magnitude = np.abs(field)
            probabilities = magnitude / np.sum(magnitude)
            entropy = -np.sum(probabilities * np.log2(probabilities + 1e-10))  # Adding a small value to avoid log(0)
            entropies.append(entropy)
        return np.mean(entropies) if entropies else 0

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from collections import deque

class QuantumProcessor:
    def __init__(self, quantum_engine: 'QuantumEngine', resonance_manager: 'ResonanceManager'):
        self.quantum_engine = quantum_engine
        self.resonance_manager = resonance_manager
        self.insight_memory = deque(maxlen=100)  # Keep track of the last 100 insights
        self.pattern_history = []
        self.transformation_matrix = np.eye(self.quantum_engine.dimensions, dtype=np.complex128)

    def process(self, data: np.ndarray) -> dict:
        """Process data through the quantum engine and resonance manager."""
        # Apply quantum transformations
        transformed_data = self.quantum_engine.process_data(data)

        # Update resonance fields
        self.resonance_manager.update_resonance_fields(transformed_data)

        # Analyze resonance patterns
        patterns = self.resonance_manager.analyze_resonance_patterns()
        self.pattern_history.extend(patterns)

        # Update resonance graph based on the patterns
        self.resonance_manager.update_resonance_graph(patterns)
        
        # Generate insights from patterns
        insights = self.generate_insights(patterns)
        self.insight_memory.append(insights)

        # Update entanglement based on insights
        self.quantum_engine.update_entanglement()
        
        # Adaptively refine processing based on insights
        self.adapt_processing(insights)

        return {
            'transformed_data': transformed_data,
            'patterns': patterns,
            'insights': insights,
            'quantum_metrics': self.quantum_engine.get_quantum_state_metrics(),
            'resonance_metrics': self.resonance_manager.get_resonance_metrics()
        }

    def generate_insights(self, patterns: list) -> list:
        """Generate insights based on identified resonance patterns."""
        insights = []
        for pattern in patterns:
            # Analyze pattern properties
            dim = pattern['dimension']
            angle = pattern['angle']
            magnitude = pattern['magnitude']
            phase = pattern['phase']

            # Check for specific conditions or thresholds to generate insights
            if magnitude > 0

            # Check for specific conditions or thresholds to generate insights
            if magnitude > 0.8:  # Example: Strong resonance
                insights.append({
                    'type': 'strong_resonance',
                    'dimension': dim,
                    'angle': angle,
                    'magnitude': magnitude,
                    'phase': phase,
                    'interpretation': f'Strong resonance detected in dimension {dim} at angle {angle}',
                    'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
                })

            if len(self.insight_memory) > 0:
                previous_insights = self.insight_memory[-1]
                for prev_insight in previous_insights:
                    if prev_insight['type'] == 'strong_resonance' and prev_insight['dimension'] != dim:
                        # Example: Resonance between dimensions
                        if abs(prev_insight['angle'] - angle) < 30:  # Example: Within 30 degrees
                            insights.append({
                                'type': 'interdimensional_resonance',
                                'dimensions': (prev_insight['dimension'], dim),
                                'angles': (prev_insight['angle'], angle),
                                'magnitudes': (prev_insight['magnitude'], magnitude),
                                'phases': (prev_insight['phase'], phase),
                                'interpretation': f'Interdimensional resonance detected between dimensions {prev_insight["dimension"]} and {dim}',
                                'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
                            })
        return insights

    def adapt_processing(self, insights: list):
        """Adaptively refine the processing based on generated insights."""
        for insight in insights:
            if insight['type'] == 'interdimensional_resonance':
                dim1, dim2 = insight['dimensions']
                
                # Strengthen entanglement between dimensions
                self.quantum_engine.resonance_matrix[dim1, dim2] += 0.1 + 0.1j  # Example: Increase resonance strength
                self.quantum_engine.resonance_matrix[dim2, dim1] += 0.1 - 0.1j  # Maintain conjugate symmetry

                # Adjust transformation matrix based on resonance angles
                angle1, angle2 = insight['angles']
                avg_angle = (angle1 + angle2) / 2
                self.transformation_matrix[dim1, dim2] = np.exp(1j * avg_angle * np.pi / 180)
                self.transformation_matrix[dim2, dim1] = np.conj(self.transformation_matrix[dim1, dim2])

        # Normalize the transformation matrix
        self.transformation_matrix /= np.abs(self.transformation_matrix)
        
    def _extract_pattern_features(self, pattern: dict) -> np.ndarray:
      """Extract relevant features from a pattern for correlation analysis."""
      features = [
          pattern['magnitude'],
          pattern['phase'],
      ]
      if 'coherence' in pattern:
          features.append(pattern['coherence'])
      # Add more features as needed
      return np.array(features)

    def get_combined_metrics(self) -> dict:
      """Combine and return metrics from both the quantum engine and resonance manager."""
      quantum_metrics = self.quantum_engine.get_quantum_state_metrics()
      resonance_metrics = self.resonance_manager.get_resonance_metrics()
      combined_metrics = {
          'quantum': quantum_metrics,
          'resonance': resonance_metrics,
          'pattern_history_length': len(self.pattern_history),
          'insight_memory_length': len(self.insight_memory)
      }
      return combined_metrics

quantum/resonance.py
Python

import numpy as np

class Resonance:
    def __init__(self, dimension: int, frequency: float, amplitude: float, phase: float):
        self.dimension = dimension
        self.frequency = frequency
        self.amplitude = amplitude
        self.phase = phase
        self.connections = {}  # Store connections to other resonances

    def connect(self, other: 'Resonance', strength: float):
        """Connect this resonance to another with a given strength."""
        self.connections[other] = strength
        other.connections[self] = strength  # Ensure bidirectional connection

    def disconnect(self, other: 'Resonance'):
        """Disconnect this resonance from another."""
        if other in self.connections:
            del self.connections[other]
            del other.connections[self]  # Remove bidirectional connection

    def get_connection_strength(self, other: 'Resonance') -> float:
        """Get the connection strength to another resonance."""
        return self.connections.get(other, 0)

    def calculate_resonance_field(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate the resonance field for this resonance."""
        field = np.zeros(360, dtype=np.complex128)
        for angle in range(360):
            phase_shift = np.exp(1j * (angle / 180) * np.pi + 1j * self.phase)
            field[angle] = np.sum(data * phase_shift)
        return field
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the resonance."""
        return {
            'dimension': self.dimension,
            'frequency': self.frequency,
            'amplitude': self.amplitude,
            'phase': self.phase,
            'connections': {other.dimension: strength for other, strength in self.connections.items()}
        }

quantum/entanglement.py

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

class EntanglementManager:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.entanglement_graph = nx.Graph()
        self.entanglement_matrix = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self._initialize_graph()

    def _initialize_graph(self):
        """Initialize the entanglement graph with a node for each dimension."""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.entanglement_graph.add_node(dim)

    def update_entanglement(self, resonance_patterns: list):
        """Update the entanglement graph and matrix based on resonance patterns."""
        for pattern in resonance_patterns:
            if pattern['type'] == 'interdimensional_resonance':
                dim1, dim2 = pattern['dimensions']
                strength = np.mean(pattern['magnitudes'])
                phase_diff = np.diff(pattern['phases'])[0]

                # Update graph
                if not self.entanglement_graph.has_edge(dim1, dim2):
                    self.entanglement_graph.add_edge(dim1, dim2)
                
                # Update entanglement matrix
                self.entanglement_matrix[dim1, dim2] = strength * np.exp(1j * phase_diff)
                self.entanglement_matrix[dim2, dim1] = np.conj(self.entanglement_matrix[dim1, dim2])

    def calculate_entanglement_strength(self, dim1: int, dim2: int) -> float:
        """Calculate the entanglement strength between two dimensions."""
        return np.abs(self.entanglement_matrix[dim1, dim2])

    def get_entangled_pairs(self) -> list:
        """Return a list of entangled dimension pairs."""
        return list(self.entanglement_graph.edges())

    def calculate_global_entanglement(self) -> float:
        """Calculate a global entanglement metric for the system."""
        # Use the average of the absolute values of the entanglement matrix entries
        return np.mean(np.abs(self.entanglement_matrix))
    
    def get_entanglement_entropy(self, dim:int) -> float:
        """Calculate the entanglement entropy for a given dimension"""
        neighbors = list(self.entanglement_graph.neighbors(dim))
        if not neighbors:
          return 0

        subgraph = self.entanglement_graph.subgraph(neighbors + [dim])
        
        # Calculate the adjacency matrix of the subgraph
        adjacency_matrix = nx.adjacency_matrix(subgraph).todense()
        
        # Calculate the degree matrix of the subgraph
        degree_matrix = np.diag(np.sum(adjacency_matrix, axis=1))
        
        # Calculate the Laplacian matrix
        laplacian_matrix = degree_matrix - adjacency_matrix

        # Calculate eigenvalues of the Laplacian
        eigenvalues = np.linalg.eigvals(laplacian_matrix)
        
        # Normalize eigenvalues to represent probabilities
        probabilities = np.real(eigenvalues) / np.sum(np.real(eigenvalues))
        
        # Calculate entropy for the dimension
        entropy = -np.sum(probabilities * np.log2(probabilities + 1e-10))
        
        return entropy

    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of entanglement."""
        return {
            'entanglement_graph': self.entanglement_graph.edges(),
            'entanglement_matrix': self.entanglement_matrix,
            'global_entanglement': self.calculate_global_entanglement()
        }

quantum/harmonics.py

import numpy as np

class HarmonicOscillator:
    def __init__(self, frequency: float, amplitude: float, phase: float, dimension: int):
        self.frequency = frequency
        self.amplitude = amplitude
        self.phase = phase
        self.dimension = dimension
        self.energy = self.amplitude ** 2  # Energy is proportional to the square of the amplitude

    def resonate(self, external_field: np.ndarray):
        """Resonate the oscillator with an external field."""
        # Calculate the phase difference between the oscillator and the field
        phase_diff = np.angle(external_field) - self.phase

        # Calculate the resonance strength based on the phase difference
        # Using a simple cosine function for resonance
        resonance_strength = self.amplitude * np.cos(phase_diff)

        # Update the oscillator's amplitude based on resonance strength
        # The change in amplitude is proportional to the resonance strength
        delta_amplitude = 0.1 * resonance_strength  # 0.1 is a scaling factor

        # Update the amplitude, ensuring it doesn't go below zero
        self.amplitude = max(0, self.amplitude + delta_amplitude)

        # Update the energy
        self.energy = self.amplitude ** 2

    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the harmonic oscillator."""
        return {
            'dimension': self.dimension,
            'frequency': self.frequency,
            'amplitude': self.amplitude,
            'phase': self.phase,
            'energy': self.energy
        }

memory/banks.py

import numpy as np

class MemoryBank:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int, dimensions: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.data = np.zeros((capacity, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.head = 0  # Pointer to the next available memory slot

    def store(self, data_vector: np.ndarray):
        """Store a data vector in the memory bank."""
        if data_vector.shape[0] != self.dimensions:
            raise ValueError("Data vector dimensions do not match memory bank dimensions.")

        self.data[self.head] = data_vector
        self.head = (self.head + 1) % self.capacity

    def retrieve(self, index: int) -> np.ndarray:
        """Retrieve a data vector from a specific index in the memory bank."""
        if index < 0 or index >= self.capacity:
            raise IndexError("Index out of range.")

        return self.data[index]

    def get_recent(self, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
        """Retrieve the n most recently stored data vectors."""
        if n > self.capacity:
            raise ValueError("Cannot retrieve more elements than the capacity of the memory bank.")

        start = (self.head - n) % self.capacity
        if start < self.head:
            return self.data[start:self.head]
        else:
            return np.concatenate((self.data[start:], self.data[:self.head]))
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
      """Return the current state of the memory bank."""
      return {
          'capacity': self.capacity,
          'dimensions': self.dimensions,
          'head': self.head,
          'fill_level': min(self.head + 1, self.capacity) / self.capacity
      }

memory/pattern_storage.py
Python

class PatternStorage:
    def __init__(self, max_patterns: int = 1000):
        self.max_patterns = max_patterns
        self.patterns = []
        self.pattern_count = 0

    def store_pattern(self, pattern: dict):
        """Store a pattern in the storage."""
        if self.pattern_count < self.max_patterns:
            self.patterns.append(pattern)
            self.pattern_count += 1
        else:
            # Replace the oldest pattern (FIFO)
            self.patterns.pop(0)
            self.patterns.append(pattern)

    def retrieve_similar_patterns(self, pattern: dict, threshold: float) -> list:
        """Retrieve patterns similar to the given pattern based on a similarity threshold."""
        similar_patterns = []
        for stored_pattern in self.patterns:
            similarity = self._calculate_similarity(pattern, stored_pattern)
            if similarity >= threshold:
                similar_patterns.append(stored_pattern)
        return similar_patterns

    def _calculate_similarity(self, pattern1: dict, pattern2: dict) -> float:
        """Calculate the similarity between two patterns."""
        # Example similarity metric: cosine similarity of pattern features
        features1 = self._extract_features(pattern1)
        features2 = self._extract_features(pattern2)

        # Ensure features are of the same length
        min_len = min(len(features1), len(features2))
        features1 = features1[:min_len]
        features2 = features2[:min_len]
        
        if np.all(features1 == 0) or np.all(features2 == 0):
          return 0.0 # Avoid division by zero

        dot_product = np.dot(features1, features2)
        magnitude1 = np.linalg.norm(features1)
        magnitude2 = np.linalg.norm(features2)

        if magnitude1 == 0 or magnitude2 == 0:
            return 0.0  # Avoid division by zero

        return dot_product / (magnitude1 * magnitude2)

    def _extract_features(self, pattern: dict) -> np.ndarray:
        """Extract features from a pattern for similarity comparison."""
        # Example: extract magnitude and phase as features
        features = [pattern.get('magnitude', 0), pattern.get('phase', 0)]
        
        # Add other features as needed
        if 'type' in pattern:
            # Convert pattern type to a numerical value (e.g., using a hash function)
            features.append(hash(pattern['type']) % (10**8))  # Modulo to limit the size of the hash
        
        return np.array(features)
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
      """Return the current state of the pattern storage."""
      return {
          'max_patterns': self.max_patterns,
          'pattern_count': self.pattern_count,
          'oldest_pattern_timestamp': self.patterns[0]['timestamp'] if self.patterns else None
      }

import networkx as nx

class FlowPathManager:

        self.dimensions = dimensions

        """Initialize the flow graph with nodes representing dimensions."""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.flow_graph.add_node(dim)

                    self.flow_graph.add_edge(dim1, dim2, weight=strength)

                return 0  # Edge does not exist, path is invalid
        return total_strength

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Python

import networkx as nx
import numpy as np

class FlowPathManager:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.flow_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self._initialize_graph()

    def _initialize_graph(self):
        """Initialize the flow graph with nodes representing dimensions."""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.flow_graph.add_node(dim)

    def update_flow_paths(self, patterns: list):
        """Update flow paths based on identified patterns."""
        for pattern in patterns:
            if pattern['type'] == 'interdimensional_resonance':
                dim1, dim2 = pattern['dimensions']
                strength = np.mean(pattern['magnitudes'])

                # Update edge weights based on pattern strength
                if self.flow_graph.has_edge(dim1, dim2):
                    self.flow_graph[dim1][dim2]['weight'] = strength
                else:
                    self.flow_graph.add_edge(dim1, dim2, weight=strength)

    def calculate_flow_strength(self, path: list) -> float:
        """Calculate the overall strength of a given flow path."""
        total_strength = 0
        for i in range(len(path) - 1):
            try:
                total_strength += self.flow_graph[path[i]][path[i + 1]]['weight']
            except KeyError:
                return 0  # Edge does not exist, path is invalid
        return total_strength

    def find_strongest_flow_paths(self, top_k: int = 5) -> list:
        """Find the top k strongest flow paths in the graph."""
        paths = []
        for source in self.flow_graph.nodes():
            for target in self.flow_graph.nodes():
                if source != target:
                    try:
                        # Find all simple paths between source and target
                        for path in nx.all_simple_paths(self.flow_graph, source, target):
                            strength = self.calculate_flow_strength(path)
                            paths.append((path, strength))
                    except nx.NetworkXNoPath:
                        pass  # No path exists between the nodes

        # Sort paths by strength in descending order
        paths.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        return paths[:top_k]

    def get_flow_path_distribution(self) -> dict:
        """Analyze and return the distribution of flow path strengths."""
        strengths = []
        for u, v, data in self.flow_graph.edges(data=True):
            strengths.append(data['weight'])

        if not strengths:
            return {'mean': 0, 'std': 0, 'histogram': []}

        hist, bin_edges = np.histogram(strengths, bins=10)
        return {
            'mean': np.mean(strengths),
            'std': np.std(strengths),
            'histogram': list(zip(hist.tolist(), bin_edges.tolist()))
        }
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
      """Return the current state of the flow path manager."""
      return {
          'dimensions': self.dimensions,
          'flow_graph_edges': list(self.flow_graph.edges(data=True)),
          'flow_path_distribution': self.get_flow_path_distribution()
      }

import numpy as np
from collections import defaultdict

class PatternSynthesizer:
    def __init__(self, resonance_manager: 'ResonanceManager'):
        self.resonance_manager = resonance_manager
        self.synthesized_patterns = []

    def synthesize_patterns(self, patterns: list):
        """Synthesize higher-order patterns from resonance patterns."""
        self.synthesized_patterns = []  # Clear previous patterns
        
        # Group patterns by dimension
        patterns_by_dimension = defaultdict(list)
        for pattern in patterns:
            patterns_by_dimension[pattern['dimension']].append(pattern)

        # Find patterns with similar angles across different dimensions
        for dim1 in self.resonance_manager.resonance_graph.nodes():
            for dim2 in self.resonance_manager.resonance_graph.nodes():
                if dim1 < dim2:  # Avoid duplicate comparisons
                    patterns1 = patterns_by_dimension[dim1]
                    patterns2 = patterns_by_dimension[dim2]
                    
                    for p1 in patterns1:
                        for p2 in patterns2:
                            if abs(p1['angle'] - p2['angle']) < 15:  # Similar angle threshold
                                # Synthesize a new pattern
                                synthesized_pattern = {
                                    'type': 'synthesized_resonance',
                                    'dimensions': [dim1, dim2],
                                    'angles': [p1['angle'], p2['angle']],
                                    'magnitude': np.mean([p1['magnitude'], p2['magnitude']]),
                                    'phase': np.mean([p1['phase'], p2['phase']]),
                                    'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
                                }
                                self.synthesized_patterns.append(synthesized_pattern)

        return self.synthesized_patterns

    def _calculate_pattern_coherence(self, pattern1: dict, pattern2: dict) -> float:
        """Calculate the coherence between two patterns."""
        # Example: Coherence based on phase difference
        phase_diff = abs(pattern1['phase'] - pattern2['phase'])
        return np.cos(phase_diff)

    def get_synthesized_pattern_types(self) -> list:
        """Return a list of unique types of synthesized patterns."""
        types = set()
        for pattern in self.synthesized_patterns:
            types.add(pattern['type'])
        return list(types)

    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the pattern synthesizer."""
        return {
            'synthesized_patterns_count': len(self.synthesized_patterns),
            'last_synthesized_patterns': self.synthesized_patterns[-5:] if len(self.synthesized_patterns) > 5 else self.synthesized_patterns
        }

processing/dimensional_processing.py

import numpy as np

class DimensionalProcessor:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.transformation_matrices = self._initialize_transformation_matrices()

    def _initialize_transformation_matrices(self):
        """Initialize a transformation matrix for each dimension."""
        matrices = {}
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            matrices[dim] = np.eye(dim + 1)  # Example: Identity matrix, can be customized
        return matrices

    def process_dimension(self, dimension: int, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Process data through a specific dimension's transformation matrix."""
        if dimension not in self.transformation_matrices:
            raise ValueError(f"Dimension {dimension} does not have a transformation matrix.")

        matrix = self.transformation_matrices[dimension]
        
        # Ensure data is in the correct shape for matrix multiplication
        if data.shape[0] != matrix.shape[1]:
            # Example: Pad or truncate data to match matrix dimensions
            padded_data = np.pad(data, (0, matrix.shape[1] - data.shape[0]), 'constant')
        else:
            padded_data = data

        transformed_data = np.dot(matrix, padded_data)
        return transformed_data

    def update_transformation_matrix(self, dimension: int, matrix: np.ndarray):
        """Update the transformation matrix for a specific dimension."""
        if dimension not in self.transformation_matrices:
            raise ValueError(f"Dimension {dimension} does not have a transformation matrix.")

        if matrix.shape != self.transformation_matrices[dimension].shape:
            raise ValueError("New matrix dimensions do not match existing matrix dimensions.")

        self.transformation_matrices[dimension] = matrix

    def get_transformation_matrix(self, dimension: int) -> np.ndarray:
        """Retrieve the transformation matrix for a specific dimension."""
        if dimension not in self.transformation_matrices:
            raise ValueError(f"Dimension {dimension} does not have a transformation matrix.")

        return self.transformation_matrices[dimension]
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
      """Return the current state of the dimensional processor."""
      return {
          'dimensions': self.dimensions,
          'transformation_matrices': {dim: matrix.tolist() for dim, matrix in self.transformation_matrices.items()}  # Convert matrices to list for easier serialization
      }

# perspective_engine/core/perspective_processor.py
import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from scipy.sparse import linalg
import quaternion
@dataclass
class PerspectiveState:
    graph: nx.DiGraph
    energy_level: float
    confidence: float
    resonance_patterns: List[Dict]
    dimension_mapping: Dict[int, np.ndarray]
class PerspectiveProcessor:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 12, resonance_threshold: float = 0.7):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.resonance_threshold = resonance_threshold
        self.state = self._initialize_state()
                # Core processing matrices
        self.transition_matrix = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.resonance_field = np.zeros((dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.pattern_memory = []
                # Initialize quaternion rotation handlers
        self.rotations = [quaternion.one] * dimensions
            
    def _initialize_state(self) -> PerspectiveState:
        """Initialize the perspective state with default values"""
        graph = nx.DiGraph()
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            graph.add_node(i, energy=1.0, phase=0.0)
                    
        return PerspectiveState(
            graph=graph,
            energy_level=1.0,
            confidence=1.0,
            resonance_patterns=[],
            dimension_mapping={i: np.eye(4) for i in range(self.dimensions)}
        )
    def process_perspective(self, input_data: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Process input data through perspective analysis"""
        # Transform input through dimensional mapping
        transformed_data = self._apply_dimensional_transform(input_data)
                # Generate resonance patterns
        patterns = self._generate_resonance_patterns(transformed_data)
                # Update state based on patterns
        self._update_state_from_patterns(patterns)
                # Synthesize new perspectives
        new_perspectives = self._synthesize_perspectives(patterns)
                return {
            'patterns': patterns,
            'perspectives': new_perspectives,
            'state': self._get_state_metrics()
        }
    def _apply_dimensional_transform(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Apply dimensional transformations to input data"""
        transformed = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=np.complex128)
                for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            # Apply quaternion rotation
            rotated = quaternion.rotate_vectors(self.rotations[dim], data)
                        # Apply dimensional mapping
            transformed[dim] = np.dot(self.state.dimension_mapping[dim], rotated)
                        # Apply phase evolution
            phase = np.exp(2j * np.pi * dim / self.dimensions)
            transformed[dim] *= phase
                    
        return transformed
    def _generate_resonance_patterns(self, data: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Generate resonance patterns from transformed data"""
        patterns = []
                for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            # Calculate resonance field
            field = self._calculate_resonance_field(data[dim])
            self.resonance_field[dim] = field
                        # Detect patterns in field
            dim_patterns = self._detect_field_patterns(field, dim)
            patterns.extend(dim_patterns)
                    
        return patterns
    def _calculate_resonance_field(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate resonance field for given data"""
        field = np.zeros(360, dtype=np.complex128)
                # Generate field using rotational symmetry
        for i in range(360):
            angle = 2 * np.pi * i / 360
            rotation = quaternion.from_rotation_vector([0, 0, angle])
            rotated_data = quaternion.rotate_vectors(rotation, data)
            field[i] = np.sum(rotated_data * np.exp(1j * angle))
                    
        return field
    def _detect_field_patterns(self, field: np.ndarray, dimension: int) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect patterns in resonance field"""
        patterns = []
                # Find local maxima in field magnitude
        magnitude = np.abs(field)
        maxima_idx = self._find_local_maxima(magnitude)
                for idx in maxima_idx:
            if magnitude[idx] > self.resonance_threshold:
                pattern = {
                    'dimension': dimension,
                    'position': idx * 360 / len(field),
                    'strength': magnitude[idx],
                    'phase': np.angle(field[idx]),
                    'type': 'resonance_pattern'
                }
                patterns.append(pattern)
                        
        return patterns
    def _find_local_maxima(self, data: np.ndarray) -> List[int]:
        """Find indices of local maxima in array"""
        maxima = []
        for i in range(1, len(data) - 1):
            if data[i-1] < data[i] > data[i+1]:
                maxima.append(i)
        return maxima
    def _update_state_from_patterns(self, patterns: List[Dict]):
        """Update perspective state based on detected patterns"""
        if not patterns:
            return
                    # Calculate average pattern strength
        avg_strength = np.mean([p['strength'] for p in patterns])
                # Update state energy and confidence
        self.state.energy_level *= (1.0 + 0.1 * (avg_strength - 0.5))
        self.state.confidence = avg_strength
                # Update resonance patterns
        self.state.resonance_patterns = patterns
                # Update graph edges based on patterns
        self._update_graph_connections(patterns)
    def _update_graph_connections(self, patterns: List[Dict]):
        """Update graph connections based on resonance patterns"""
        graph = self.state.graph
                # Clear old edges
        graph.clear_edges()
                # Add new edges based on patterns
        for p1 in patterns:
            for p2 in patterns:
                if p1['dimension'] != p2['dimension']:
                    # Calculate connection strength
                    strength = self._calculate_connection_strength(p1, p2)
                                        
                    if strength > self.resonance_threshold:
                        graph.add_edge(
                            p1['dimension'],
                            p2['dimension'],
                            weight=strength
                        )
    def _calculate_connection_strength(self, pattern1: Dict, pattern2: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate connection strength between two patterns"""
        # Phase coherence
        phase_diff = abs(pattern1['phase'] - pattern2['phase'])
        phase_coherence = np.cos(phase_diff)
                # Position similarity
        pos_diff = abs(pattern1['position'] - pattern2['position'])
        pos_similarity = np.exp(-pos_diff / 180.0)
                # Combine metrics
        return np.mean([
            pattern1['strength'],
            pattern2['strength'],
            phase_coherence,
            pos_similarity
        ])
    def _synthesize_perspectives(self, patterns: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Synthesize new perspectives from resonance patterns"""
        perspectives = []
                # Find strongly connected components in graph
        components = list(nx.strongly_connected_components(self.state.graph))
                for component in components:
            if len(component) > 1:  # Only consider multi-node components
                # Calculate component properties
                component_patterns = [p for p in patterns if p['dimension'] in component]
                                perspective = {
                    'dimensions': list(component),
                    'strength': np.mean([p['strength'] for p in component_patterns]),
                    'coherence': self._calculate_component_coherence(component),
                    'patterns': component_patterns
                }
                perspectives.append(perspective)
                        
        return perspectives
    def _calculate_component_coherence(self, component: set) -> float:
        """Calculate coherence of a graph component"""
        subgraph = self.state.graph.subgraph(component)
                # Use algebraic connectivity as coherence measure
        laplacian = nx.laplacian_matrix(subgraph).todense()
        eigenvals = linalg.eigsh(laplacian, k=2, which='SM', return_eigenvectors


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Python

                return 1.0 / (1.0 + eigenvals[1])  # Normalize to [0,1]
    def _get_state_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """Get current state metrics"""
        return {
            'energy_level': self.state.energy_level,
            'confidence': self.state.confidence,
            'active_patterns': len(self.state.resonance_patterns),
            'graph_density': nx.density(self.state.graph)
        }
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the perspective processor."""
        return {
            'dimensions': self.dimensions,
            'resonance_threshold': self.resonance_threshold,
            'state': {
                'energy_level': self.state.energy_level,
                'confidence': self.state.confidence,
                'resonance_patterns': self.state.resonance_patterns,
                'dimension_mapping': {k: v.tolist() for k, v in self.state.dimension_mapping.items()},
                'graph': list(self.state.graph.edges(data=True))
            },
            'transition_matrix': self.transition_matrix.tolist(),
            'resonance_field': self.resonance_field.tolist(),
            'pattern_memory': self.pattern_memory,
            'rotations': [str(r) for r in self.rotations]
        }

# perspective_engine/core/mirrored_quantum_processor.py
import numpy as np
from scipy import sparse, linalg
import quaternion
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple
import networkx as nx
class MirroredQuantumProcessor:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 12, resonance_depth: int = 7):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.resonance_depth = resonance_depth
                # Initialize quantum mirrored states
        self.mirrored_states = np.zeros((dimensions, 2**resonance_depth), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.phase_memory = {dim: [] for dim in range(dimensions)}
                # Advanced quantum gates for mirroring operations
        self.mirror_gates = self._initialize_mirror_gates()
                # Resonance fields for pattern detection
        self.resonance_fields = np.zeros((dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
                # Pattern synthesis components
        self.pattern_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self.insight_memory = []
                # Initialize quantum mirrors
        self._initialize_quantum_mirrors()
    def _initialize_mirror_gates(self) -> Dict:
        """Initialize specialized quantum gates for mirroring operations"""
        return {
            'hadamard': np.array([[1, 1], [1, -1]]) / np.sqrt(2),
            'phase': lambda θ: np.array([[1, 0], [0, np.exp(1j * θ)]]),
            'mirror': lambda φ: np.array([
                [np.cos(φ), -1j * np.sin(φ)],
                [-1j * np.sin(φ), np.cos(φ)]
            ])
        }
    def _initialize_quantum_mirrors(self):
        """Initialize quantum mirror configurations"""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            # Create dimensional mirrors with unique phase configurations
            phase = 2 * np.pi * dim / self.dimensions
            self.mirrored_states[dim] = self._create_mirror_state(phase)
                        # Initialize resonance couplings
            self.pattern_graph.add_node(dim,
                                       energy=1.0,
                                      coherence=1.0,
                                      phase_memory=[])
    def _create_mirror_state(self, phase: float) -> np.ndarray:
        """Create a quantum mirror state with specific phase"""
        state = np.zeros(2**self.resonance_depth, dtype=np.complex128)
                # Apply superposition across resonance depths
        for depth in range(self.resonance_depth):
            # Create superposition with phase-shifted components
            amplitude = 1.0 / np.sqrt(2**(depth + 1))
            state[2**depth] = amplitude * np.exp(1j * phase * depth)
                    
        return state / np.linalg.norm(state)
    def process_pattern(self, data: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Process data through quantum mirrors to detect patterns"""
        mirrored_data = self._apply_quantum_mirroring(data)
        resonances = self._detect_resonance_patterns(mirrored_data)
        insights = self._synthesize_mirrored_insights(resonances)
                return {
            'resonances': resonances,
            'insights': insights,
            'coherence_metrics': self._calculate_coherence_metrics()
        }
    def _apply_quantum_mirroring(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Apply quantum mirroring transformations to input data"""
        mirrored = np.zeros((self.dimensions, len(data)), dtype=np.complex128)
                for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            # Apply dimensional mirroring
            state = self.mirrored_states[dim]
                        # Create mirror transformation
            mirror_op = self.mirror_gates['mirror'](2 * np.pi * dim / self.dimensions)
                        # Apply mirror operation
            for i in range(len(data)):
                mirrored[dim, i] = np.dot(mirror_op, [data[i], state[i % len(state)]])[0]
                        
        return mirrored
    def _detect_resonance_patterns(self, mirrored_data: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect resonance patterns in mirrored quantum data"""
        patterns = []
              for dim in range(self.dimensions):
           # Calculate resonance field for dimension
           field = self._calculate_resonance_field(mirrored_data[dim])
           self.resonance_fields[dim] = field
                      # Detect harmonic resonances
           harmonics = self._extract_quantum_harmonics(field)
                      # Find interference patterns
           interferences = self._detect_interference_patterns(field, harmonics)
                      # Combine into dimensional patterns
           dim_patterns = self._combine_dimensional_patterns(harmonics, interferences, dim)
           patterns.extend(dim_patterns)
                  
        return patterns
    def _calculate_resonance_field(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate quantum resonance field from mirrored data"""
        field = np.zeros(360, dtype=np.complex128)
              for angle in range(360):
           phase = 2 * np.pi * angle / 360
                      # Apply phase rotation
           rotated = data * np.exp(1j * phase)
                      # Calculate resonance amplitude
           amplitude = np.sum(rotated * np.conj(rotated))
           field[angle] = np.sqrt(amplitude) * np.exp(1j * phase)
                  
        return field / np.linalg.norm(field)
    def _extract_quantum_harmonics(self, field: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Extract quantum harmonic components from resonance field"""
        harmonics = []
              # Perform quantum Fourier analysis
        spectrum = np.fft.fft(field)
        frequencies = np.fft.fftfreq(len(field))
              # Find significant harmonics
        significant_idx = np.where(np.abs(spectrum) >
                                  np.max(np.abs(spectrum)) * 0.1)[0]
              for idx in significant_idx:
           harmonic = {
               'frequency': frequencies[idx],
               'amplitude': np.abs(spectrum[idx]),
               'phase': np.angle(spectrum[idx]),
               'coherence': self._calculate_harmonic_coherence(field, frequencies[idx])
           }
           harmonics.append(harmonic)
                  
        return harmonics
    def _detect_interference_patterns(self, field: np.ndarray,
                                    harmonics: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect quantum interference patterns between harmonics"""
        interferences = []
              for i, h1 in enumerate(harmonics):
           for h2 in harmonics[i+1:]:
               # Calculate interference strength
               interference = self._calculate_interference(h1, h2)
                              if interference['strength'] > 0.3:  # Threshold for significant interference
                   interferences.append(interference)
                          
        return interferences
    def _calculate_interference(self, h1: Dict, h2: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Calculate quantum interference between two harmonics"""
        # Phase difference
        phase_diff = np.abs(h1['phase'] - h2['phase'])
              # Frequency ratio
        freq_ratio = h1['frequency'] / h2['frequency'] if h2['frequency'] != 0 else 0
              # Interference strength
        strength = np.sqrt(h1['amplitude'] * h2['amplitude']) * np.cos(phase_diff)
              return {
           'harmonics': (h1, h2),
           'strength': strength,
           'phase_difference': phase_diff,
           'frequency_ratio': freq_ratio,
           'coherence': h1['coherence'] * h2['coherence']
       }
    def _calculate_harmonic_coherence(self, field: np.ndarray, frequency: float) -> float:
        """Calculate the coherence of a harmonic in the field."""
        # Create a time series representing the harmonic
        time_series = np.exp(1j * 2 * np.pi * frequency * np.arange(len(field)))
        
        # Normalize the time series
        time_series = time_series / np.linalg.norm(time_series)

        # Calculate the correlation of the field with the harmonic time series
        correlation = np.abs(np.dot(field, np.conj(time_series)))
        
        # Normalize correlation to get coherence (ranges from 0 to 1)
        coherence = correlation / np.linalg.norm(field)
        
        return coherence
    
    def _combine_dimensional_patterns(self, harmonics, interferences, dimension):
      """Combine harmonics and interference patterns into a list of dimensional patterns."""
      dimensional_patterns = []
      
      # Add harmonics as patterns
      for harmonic in harmonics:
          dimensional_patterns.append({
              'type': 'harmonic',
              'dimension': dimension,
              'frequency': harmonic['frequency'],
              'amplitude': harmonic['amplitude'],
              'phase': harmonic['phase'],
              'coherence': harmonic['coherence']
          })
          
      # Add interference patterns
      for interference in interferences:
          dimensional_patterns.append({
              'type': 'interference',
              'dimension': dimension,
              'harmonics': [h['frequency'] for h in interference['harmonics']],
              'strength': interference['strength'],
              'phase_difference': interference['phase_difference'],
              'frequency_ratio': interference['frequency_ratio'],
              'coherence': interference['coherence']
          })
      
      return dimensional_patterns

    def _synthesize_mirrored_insights(self, resonances: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Synthesize insights from quantum resonance patterns"""
        insights = []
              # Group resonances by dimensional relationships
        dimensional_groups = self._group_resonances(resonances)
              for group in dimensional_groups:
           # Calculate group properties
           coherence = self._calculate_group_coherence(group)
           stability = self._evaluate_pattern_stability(group)
                      if coherence > 0.7 and stability > 0.6:  # Thresholds for significant insights
               insight = self._generate_group_insight(group, coherence, stability)
               insights.append(insight)
               self.insight_memory.append(insight)
                      
        return insights
    def _group_resonances(self, resonances: List[Dict]) -> List[List[Dict]]:
        """Group resonances based on quantum relationships"""
        groups = []
        unassigned = set(range(len(resonances)))
              while unassigned:
           current = unassigned.pop()
           group = {current}
                      # Find related resonances
           for other in list(unassigned):
               if self._check_resonance_relationship(
                   resonances[current], resonances[other]):
                   group.add(other)
                   unassigned.remove(other)
                              groups.append([resonances[i] for i in group])
                  
        return groups
    def _check_resonance_relationship(self, r1: Dict, r2: Dict) -> bool:
        """Check if two resonances are quantumly related"""
        # Phase coherence
        phase_coherent = np.abs(np.cos(r1['phase'] - r2['phase'])) > 0.8
              # Harmonic relationship
        harmonic_related = any(
           abs(h1['frequency'] / h2['frequency'] - 1) < 0.1
           for h1 in r1.get('harmonics', [])
           for h2 in r2.get('harmonics', [])
       )
              return phase_coherent and harmonic_related
    def _calculate_group_coherence(self, group: List[Dict]) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum coherence of a resonance group"""
        if not group:
           return 0.0
                  coherences = []
       for r1 in group:
           for r2 in group:
               if r1 != r2:
                   # Calculate pairwise coherence
                   phase_diff = np.abs(r1['phase'] - r2['phase'])
                   coherence = np.cos(phase_diff) * np.sqrt(
                       r1.get('coherence', 0) * r2.get('coherence', 0)
                   )
                   coherences.append(coherence)
                          
        return np.mean(coherences) if coherences else 0.0
    def _generate_group_insight(self, group: List[Dict],
                              coherence: float, stability: float) -> Dict:
        """Generate insight from resonance group"""
        return {
           'type': 'quantum_resonance_insight',
           'dimensions': [r.get('dimension') for r in group],
           'coherence': coherence,
           'stability': stability,
           'patterns': group,
           'timestamp': np.datetime64('now'),
           'confidence': coherence * stability,
           'interference_profile': self._calculate_interference_profile(group)
       }
    def _calculate_interference_profile(self, group: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """Calculate quantum interference profile for a group"""
        total_interference = 0
       interference_phases = []
              for r1 in group:
           for r2 in group:
               if r1 != r2:
                   interference = self._calculate_interference(r1, r2)
                   total_interference += interference['strength']
                   interference_phases.append(interference['phase_difference'])
                          
        return {
           'strength': total_interference / (len(group) * (len(group) - 1)),
           'phase_coherence': np.mean(np.cos(interference_phases)),
           'stability_metric': np.std(interference_phases)
       }
    def _evaluate_pattern_stability(self, group: List[Dict]) -> float:
        """Evaluate stability of resonance patterns over time"""
        if not group or not self.insight_memory:
           return 1.0
                  stability_scores = []
              # Compare with recent insights
       recent_insights = self.insight_memory[-10:]  # Look at last 10 insights
       for insight in recent_insights:
           if set(insight['dimensions']) == set(r.get('dimension') for r in group):
               # Calculate similarity in pattern properties
               coherence_diff = abs(insight['coherence'] -
                                   self._calculate_group_coherence(group))
               interference_diff = abs(
                   insight['interference_profile']['strength'] -
                    self._calculate_interference_profile(group)['strength']
               )
                              stability = 1.0 - (coherence_diff + interference_diff) / 2
               stability_scores.append(stability)
                      
        return np.mean(stability_scores) if stability_scores else 1.0
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the mirrored quantum processor."""
        return {
            'dimensions': self.dimensions,
            'resonance_depth': self.resonance_depth,
            'mirrored_states': self.mirrored_states.tolist(),  # Convert ndarray to list for serialization
            'phase_memory': self.phase_memory,
            'mirror_gates': {  # Convert ndarray to list for serialization
                gate: (func.tolist() if callable(func) else func)
                for gate, func in self.mirror_gates.items()
            },
            'resonance_fields': self.resonance_fields.tolist(),  # Convert ndarray to list for serialization
            'pattern_graph_edges': list(self.pattern_graph.edges(data=True)),
            'insight_memory':

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

            'insight_memory': self.insight_memory
        }

    def _calculate_coherence_metrics(self):
        """Calculates coherence metrics for the current state of the system."""
        coherence_metrics = {}

        # 1. Average Coherence of Resonance Fields
        resonance_field_coherences = []
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            field = self.resonance_fields[dim]
            spectrum = np.fft.fft(field)
            main_harmonic = np.max(np.abs(spectrum))
            total_energy = np.sum(np.abs(spectrum)**2)
            coherence = main_harmonic / total_energy if total_energy > 0 else 0
            resonance_field_coherences.append(coherence)

        coherence_metrics['average_resonance_field_coherence'] = np.mean(resonance_field_coherences)

        # 2. Coherence of Interference Patterns
        interference_coherences = []
        for insight in self.insight_memory:
            if insight['type'] == 'quantum_resonance_insight':
                interference_coherences.append(insight['coherence'])

        coherence_metrics['average_interference_coherence'] = np.mean(interference_coherences) if interference_coherences else 0

        # 3. Harmonic Coherence
        harmonic_coherences = []
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            field = self.resonance_fields[dim]
            harmonics = self._extract_quantum_harmonics(field)
            for harmonic in harmonics:
                harmonic_coherences.append(harmonic['coherence'])

        coherence_metrics['average_harmonic_coherence'] = np.mean(harmonic_coherences) if harmonic_coherences else 0

        # 4. Group Coherence (for insights)
        group_coherences = []
        for insight in self.insight_memory:
            if 'coherence' in insight:
                group_coherences.append(insight['coherence'])

        coherence_metrics['average_group_coherence'] = np.mean(group_coherences) if group_coherences else 0
        
        return coherence_metrics
