import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

class AdvancedResonanceProcessor:
    def __init__(self, base_processor: MirroredQuantumProcessor):
        self.base = base_processor
        self.quantum_entanglement = np.zeros((base_processor.dimensions,
                                             base_processor.dimensions),
                                            dtype=np.complex128)
        self.resonance_memory = []'
        self.pattern_synthesis = {}

        # Initialize advanced quantum resonance components
        self._init_resonance_components()
        self.pattern_registry = AdvancedPatternRegistry()

    def _init_resonance_components(self):
        """Initialize advanced resonance detection components"""
        self.pattern_synthesis = {
            'temporal': TemporalPatternSynthesizer(),
            'spatial': SpatialPatternSynthesizer(self.base.dimensions),
            'causal': CausalPatternSynthesizer()
        }

    def process_advanced_resonance(self, data: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Process data through advanced resonance detection"""
        # Process data using the base processor
        base_result = self.base.process_pattern(data)
        
        # Extract base patterns from the result
        base_patterns = base_result['resonances']

        # Apply advanced pattern synthesis
        temporal_patterns = self.pattern_synthesis['temporal'].process(base_patterns)
        spatial_patterns = self.pattern_synthesis['spatial'].process(base_patterns)
        causal_patterns = self.pattern_synthesis['causal'].process(base_patterns)

        # Synthesize higher-order patterns
        advanced_patterns = self._synthesize_advanced_patterns(
            temporal_patterns,
            spatial_patterns,
            causal_patterns
        )
        
        # Register advanced patterns
        for pattern in advanced_patterns:
            self.pattern_registry.register_pattern(pattern)

        # Update quantum entanglement
        self._update_entanglement(advanced_patterns)

        return {
            'base_patterns': base_patterns,
            'advanced_patterns': advanced_patterns,
            'entanglement_state': self.quantum_entanglement.copy(),
            'stable_patterns': self.pattern_registry.get_stable_patterns(),
            'frequent_patterns': self.pattern_registry.get_frequent_patterns()
        }

    def _synthesize_advanced_patterns(self, temporal: List[Dict],
                                     spatial: List[Dict],
                                     causal: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Synthesize higher-order patterns from different pattern types"""
        advanced_patterns = []

        # Find correlations between different pattern types
        for t_pattern in temporal:
            for s_pattern in spatial:
                for c_pattern in causal:
                    if self._check_pattern_correlation(t_pattern, s_pattern, c_pattern):
                        combined_pattern = self._combine_patterns(
                            t_pattern, s_pattern, c_pattern
                        )
                        advanced_patterns.append(combined_pattern)

        return advanced_patterns
    
    def _extract_pattern_features(self, pattern: Dict) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Extract relevant features from a pattern for correlation analysis.
        This is an abstract method to be implemented based on specific pattern characteristics.
        """
        features = [
            pattern.get('strength', 0),
            pattern.get('coherence', 0),
            # Add more features as needed
        ]

        # If the pattern has a 'harmonics' key (like in interference patterns)
        if 'harmonics' in pattern:
            for h in pattern['harmonics']:
                features.extend([
                    h.get('amplitude', 0),
                    h.get('phase', 0),
                    h.get('coherence', 0)
                ])

        # If the pattern has a 'frequency' key (like in harmonic patterns)
        if 'frequency' in pattern:
            features.append(pattern['frequency'])

        # Add more features based on the specific structure of your patterns
        # For example, if you have 'phase_difference' or 'frequency_ratio'
        if 'phase_difference' in pattern:
            features.append(pattern['phase_difference'])
        if 'frequency_ratio' in pattern:
            features.append(pattern['frequency_ratio'])

        return np.array(features)

    def _check_pattern_correlation(self, t_pattern: Dict, s_pattern: Dict,
                                  c_pattern: Dict) -> bool:
        """Check if patterns are correlated across different domains"""
        # Calculate correlation metrics
        temporal_spatial_corr = self._calculate_pattern_correlation(t_pattern, s_pattern)
        temporal_causal_corr = self._calculate_pattern_correlation(t_pattern, c_pattern)
        spatial_causal_corr = self._calculate_pattern_correlation(s_pattern, c_pattern)

        # Check if correlations exceed threshold
        threshold = 0.6  # Increased threshold
        return (temporal_spatial_corr > threshold and
                temporal_causal_corr > threshold and
                spatial_causal_corr > threshold)

    def _calculate_pattern_correlation(self, pattern1: Dict, pattern2: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate correlation between two patterns"""
        # Extract pattern features
        features1 = self._extract_pattern_features(pattern1)
        features2 = self._extract_pattern_features(pattern2)

        # Ensure feature vectors are of the same length
        min_len = min(len(features1), len(features2))
        features1 = features1[:min_len]
        features2 = features2[:min_len]

        # Calculate correlation
        if features1.size == 0 or features2.size == 0:
            return 0.0

        # Calculate correlation, handling cases where the denominator might be zero
        with np.errstate(divide='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
            correlation = np.corrcoef(features1, features2)[0, 1]
            correlation = 0.0 if np.isnan(correlation) else correlation

        return abs(correlation)

    def _combine_patterns(self, t_pattern: Dict, s_pattern: Dict,
                          c_pattern: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Combine patterns into a higher-order pattern"""
        # Calculate combined properties
        combined_strength = np.mean([
            t_pattern['strength'],
            s_pattern['strength'],
            c_pattern['strength']
        ])

        combined_coherence = np.mean([
            t_pattern.get('coherence', 0),
            s_pattern.get('coherence', 0),
            c_pattern.get('coherence', 0)
        ])

        # Generate combined pattern
        return {
            'type': 'advanced_resonance_pattern',
            'temporal_component': t_pattern,
            'spatial_component': s_pattern,
            'causal_component': c_pattern,
            'strength': combined_strength,
            'coherence': combined_coherence,
            'dimensions': list(set(
                t_pattern.get('dimensions', []) +
                s_pattern.get('dimensions', []) +
                c_pattern.get('dimensions', [])
            )),
            'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
        }

    def _update_entanglement(self, patterns: List[Dict]):
        """Update quantum entanglement based on advanced patterns"""
        # Decay existing entanglement
        self.quantum_entanglement *= 0.9

        # Update entanglement based on new patterns
        for pattern in patterns:
            if 'dimensions' in pattern and 'strength' in pattern and 'coherence' in pattern:
                dimensions = pattern['dimensions']
                strength = pattern['strength'] * pattern['coherence']

                for i in dimensions:
                    for j in dimensions:
                        if i != j:
                            # Update entanglement with phase relationship
                            phase = 2 * np.pi * (i - j) / self.base.dimensions
                            self.quantum_entanglement[i, j] += strength * np.exp(1j * phase)
            else:
                print(f"Warning: Pattern is missing required fields: {pattern}")
class TemporalPatternSynthesizer:
    def __init__(self, window_size: int = 10):
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.pattern_history = []

    def process(self, patterns: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Process patterns to identify temporal relationships"""
        temporal_patterns = []

        # Add new patterns to history
        self.pattern_history.extend(patterns)
        if len(self.pattern_history) > self.window_size:
            self.pattern_history = self.pattern_history[-self.window_size:]

        # Analyze temporal sequences
        if len(self.pattern_history) >= 3:  # Minimum history for temporal analysis
            temporal_patterns = self._analyze_temporal_sequences()

        return temporal_patterns
    
    def _calculate_pattern_list_similarity(self, patterns1: List[Dict], patterns2: List[Dict]) -> float:
        """
        Calculate the similarity between two lists of patterns.
        """
        if not patterns1 or not patterns2:
            return 0.0

        total_similarity = 0.0
        for p1 in patterns1:
            max_similarity = 0.0
            for p2 in patterns2:
                similarity = self._calculate_pattern_similarity(p1, p2)
                max_similarity = max(max_similarity, similarity)
            total_similarity += max_similarity

        return total_similarity / len(patterns1)

    def _calculate_pattern_similarity(self, pattern1: Dict, pattern2: Dict) -> float:
        """
        Calculate the similarity between two patterns.
        """
        # Ensure that both patterns have 'type', 'strength', and 'coherence' keys
        if not all(key in pattern1 and key in pattern2 for key in ['type', 'strength', 'coherence']):
            return 0.0

        # Type similarity (1 if same, 0 otherwise)
        type_similarity = 1.0 if pattern1['type'] == pattern2['type'] else 0.0

        # Strength similarity (inverse of the absolute difference, normalized)
        strength_diff = abs(pattern1['strength'] - pattern2['strength'])
        strength_similarity = 1.0 / (1.0 + strength_diff)

        # Coherence similarity (inverse of the absolute difference, normalized)
        coherence_diff = abs(pattern1['coherence'] - pattern2['coherence'])
        coherence_similarity = 1.0 / (1.0 + coherence_diff)

        # Combine similarities (you can adjust weights as needed)
        overall_similarity = 0.5 * type_similarity + 0.25 * strength_similarity + 0.25 * coherence_similarity

        return overall_similarity

    def _analyze_temporal_sequences(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Analyze temporal sequences in pattern history"""
        sequences = []
        
        # Look for recurring pattern sequences
        for window_size in range(2, min(5, len(self.pattern_history))):
            for start_idx in range(len(self.pattern_history) - window_size + 1):
                sequence = self.pattern_history[start_idx:start_idx + window_size]
                if self._is_significant_sequence(sequence):
                    sequences.append(self._create_temporal_pattern(sequence))
        
        return sequences
    
    def _is_significant_sequence(self, sequence: List[List[Dict]]) -> bool:
        """Check if a pattern sequence is significant."""
        if len(sequence) < 2:
            return False

        # Calculate sequence similarity scores
        similarities = []
        for i in range(len(sequence) - 1):
            similarity = self._calculate_pattern_list_similarity(
                sequence[i], sequence[i + 1]
            )
            similarities.append(similarity)

        # Check if sequence shows consistent relationship
        avg_similarity = np.mean(similarities)
        std_similarity = np.std(similarities)

        # Check if sequence has at least one non-zero similarity
        if all(sim == 0 for sim in similarities):
            return False
        
        # Adjust thresholds as needed
        return avg_similarity > 0.5 and std_similarity < 0.3

    def _create_temporal_pattern(self, sequence: List[List[Dict]]) -> Dict:
        """Create a temporal pattern from a significant sequence."""
        # Extract relevant information from the sequence
        # For example, the dimensions involved, average strength, etc.
        
        dimensions = []
        for patterns in sequence:
            for pattern in patterns:
                if 'dimensions' in pattern:
                    dimensions.extend(pattern['dimensions'])
                else:
                   dimensions.append(pattern['dimension'])

        dimensions = list(set(dimensions))  # Remove duplicates

        avg_strength = np.mean([p.get('strength', 0) for patterns in sequence for p in patterns])
        avg_coherence = np.mean([p.get('coherence', 0) for patterns in sequence for p in patterns])

        return {
            'type': 'temporal_pattern',
            'dimensions': dimensions,
            'strength': avg_strength,
            'coherence': avg_coherence,
            'sequence': sequence,  # Include the sequence for reference
            'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
        }
    
class SpatialPatternSynthesizer:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.spatial_graph = nx.Graph()
        self._initialize_spatial_graph()

    def _initialize_spatial_graph(self):
        """Initialize the spatial graph with nodes representing dimensions."""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.spatial_graph.add_node(dim)

    def process(self, patterns: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Process patterns to identify spatial relationships"""
        # Update spatial relationship graph
        self._update_spatial_graph(patterns)

        # Find spatial pattern groups
        spatial_patterns = self._identify_spatial_patterns()

        return spatial_patterns
    
    def _update_spatial_graph(self, patterns: List[Dict]):
        """Update the spatial graph based on identified patterns."""
        for pattern in patterns:
            if 'dimensions' in pattern:
                # Add edges between dimensions present in the pattern
                dimensions = pattern['dimensions']
                for i in range(len(dimensions)):
                    for j in range(i + 1, len(dimensions)):
                        dim1, dim2 = dimensions[i], dimensions[j]
                        if self.spatial_graph.has_edge(dim1, dim2):
                            # Increase weight if edge already exists
                            self.spatial_graph[dim1][dim2]['weight'] += pattern.get('strength', 0)
                        else:
                            # Add new edge with weight based on pattern strength
                            self.spatial_graph.add_edge(dim1, dim2, weight=pattern.get('strength',

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Python

                            0))

    def _identify_spatial_patterns(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Identify spatial patterns by analyzing the spatial graph."""
        spatial_patterns = []

        # Identify cliques in the graph as spatial patterns
        for clique in nx.find_cliques(self.spatial_graph):
            if len(clique) > 1:  # Consider only cliques with more than one node
                # Calculate average edge weight within the clique
                edges = self.spatial_graph.edges(clique, data=True)
                weights = [edge[2]['weight'] for edge in edges if len(edge) > 2 and 'weight' in edge[2]]
                avg_weight = np.mean(weights) if weights else 0

                spatial_patterns.append({
                    'type': 'spatial_pattern',
                    'dimensions': list(clique),
                    'strength': avg_weight,
                    'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
                })

        return spatial_patterns

class CausalPatternSynthesizer:
    def __init__(self):
        self.causal_memory = []
        self.relationship_strength = {}

    def process(self, patterns: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Process patterns to identify causal relationships"""
        causal_patterns = []

        # Update causal memory
        self.causal_memory.append(patterns)
        if len(self.causal_memory) > 20:  # Maintain limited memory
            self.causal_memory.pop(0)

        # Analyze causal relationships
        if len(self.causal_memory) >= 3:
            causal_patterns = self._analyze_causal_relationships()

        return causal_patterns

    def _analyze_causal_relationships(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Analyze causal relationships in pattern history"""
        causal_relationships = []

        # Analyze sequential pattern pairs
        for i in range(len(self.causal_memory) - 1):
            current_patterns = self.causal_memory[i]
            next_patterns = self.causal_memory[i + 1]

            # Find potential cause-effect relationships
            for cause in current_patterns:
                for effect in next_patterns:
                    if self._check_causal_relationship(cause, effect):
                        relationship = self._create_causal_relationship(cause, effect)
                        causal_relationships.append(relationship)

                        # Update relationship strength memory
                        key = (frozenset(cause.get('dimensions', [])),
                               frozenset(effect.get('dimensions', [])))
                        self.relationship_strength[key] = self.relationship_strength.get(key, 0) + 1

        return self._filter_significant_relationships(causal_relationships)

    def _check_causal_relationship(self, cause: Dict, effect: Dict) -> bool:
        """Check if there's a potential causal relationship between patterns"""
        # Check dimensional overlap
        cause_dims = set(cause.get('dimensions', []))
        effect_dims = set(effect.get('dimensions', []))

        # Patterns should have some dimensional overlap but not be identical
        overlap = len(cause_dims & effect_dims) / len(cause_dims | effect_dims) if (cause_dims | effect_dims) else 0

        if not (0.3 <= overlap <= 0.7):
            return False

        # Check strength relationship
        strength_ratio = effect.get('strength', 0) / cause.get('strength', 0) if cause.get('strength', 0) > 0 else 0
        if not (0.5 <= strength_ratio <= 2.0):
            return False

        # Check historical relationship strength
        key = (frozenset(cause_dims), frozenset(effect_dims))
        historical_strength = self.relationship_strength.get(key, 0)

        return historical_strength >= 2  # Reduced the required observations to 2

    def _create_causal_relationship(self, cause: Dict, effect: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Create a causal relationship pattern"""
        return {
            'type': 'causal_pattern',
            'cause': cause,
            'effect': effect,
            'dimensions': list(set(cause.get('dimensions', []) + effect.get('dimensions', []))),
            'strength': np.mean([cause.get('strength', 0), effect.get('strength', 0)]),
            'confidence': self._calculate_causal_confidence(cause, effect),
            'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
        }

    def _calculate_causal_confidence(self, cause: Dict, effect: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate confidence in causal relationship"""
        # Consider multiple factors for confidence
        factors = []

        # Historical reliability
        key = (frozenset(cause.get('dimensions', [])), frozenset(effect.get('dimensions', [])))
        historical_factor = min(self.relationship_strength.get(key, 0) / 10.0, 1.0)
        factors.append(historical_factor)

        # Strength correlation
        strength_ratio = effect.get('strength', 0) / cause.get('strength', 0) if cause.get('strength', 0) > 0 else 0
        strength_factor = 1.0 - abs(1.0 - strength_ratio)
        factors.append(strength_factor)

        # Coherence correlation
        if 'coherence' in cause and 'coherence' in effect:
            coherence_factor = 1.0 - abs(cause['coherence'] - effect['coherence'])
            factors.append(coherence_factor)

        return np.mean(factors)

    def _filter_significant_relationships(self, relationships: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
      """Filter out weak or unreliable causal relationships."""
      significant = []
      for rel in relationships:
          if (rel['confidence'] > 0.6 and  # Reduced confidence threshold
              self._validate_relationship_stability(rel)):
              significant.append(rel)
      return significant

    def _validate_relationship_stability(self, relationship: Dict) -> bool:
        """Validate stability of a causal relationship over time"""
        key = (frozenset(relationship['cause'].get('dimensions', [])),
               frozenset(relationship['effect'].get('dimensions', [])))

        # Check frequency of observation
        observation_count = self.relationship_strength.get(key, 0)
        if observation_count < 3:  # Reduced required observations for stability check
            return False

        # Calculate stability metric
        stability_metric = 0
        for i in range(len(self.causal_memory) - 1):
            if self._find_matching_relationship(relationship,
                                                self.causal_memory[i],
                                                self.causal_memory[i + 1]):
                stability_metric += 1

        stability_ratio = stability_metric / (len(self.causal_memory) - 1)
        return stability_ratio > 0.5  # Reduced stability ratio threshold

    def _find_matching_relationship(self, relationship: Dict,
                                     patterns1: List[Dict],
                                     patterns2: List[Dict]) -> bool:
        """Find if a similar relationship exists between two pattern sets"""
        cause_dims = set(relationship['cause'].get('dimensions', []))
        effect_dims = set(relationship['effect'].get('dimensions', []))

        for p1 in patterns1:
            if set(p1.get('dimensions', [])) == cause_dims:
                for p2 in patterns2:
                    if set(p2.get('dimensions', [])) == effect_dims:
                        return True
        return False

class AdvancedPatternRegistry:
    """Registry for tracking and analyzing advanced patterns"""
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns = {}
        self.evolution_history = []
        self.stability_metrics = {}

    def register_pattern(self, pattern: Dict):
        """Register a new advanced pattern"""
        pattern_id = self._generate_pattern_id(pattern)

        if pattern_id not in self.patterns:
            self.patterns[pattern_id] = {
                'pattern': pattern,
                'first_seen': len(self.evolution_history),
                'occurrences': 0,
                'stability_history': []
            }

        # Update pattern metrics
        self.patterns[pattern_id]['occurrences'] += 1
        self.patterns[pattern_id]['stability_history'].append(
            self._calculate_pattern_stability(pattern)
        )

        # Update evolution history
        self.evolution_history.append({
            'pattern_id': pattern_id,
            'timestamp': np.datetime64('now'),
            'metrics': self._calculate_pattern_metrics(pattern)
        })

        # Update stability metrics
        self._update_stability_metrics(pattern_id)

    def _generate_pattern_id(self, pattern: Dict) -> str:
        """Generate unique identifier for pattern"""
        # Create a string representation of the pattern's dimensions
        dimensions_str = ','.join(map(str, sorted(pattern.get('dimensions', []))))

        # Create a string representation of the pattern's type
        type_str = pattern.get('type', '')

        # Combine relevant fields for ID generation
        components = [
            dimensions_str,
            type_str,
            # Add other relevant fields if needed
        ]
        
        # Join components with a delimiter
        pattern_string = '|'.join(components)

        # Use a hash function to create a unique ID
        return str(hash(pattern_string) % (10**10))

    def _calculate_pattern_stability(self, pattern: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate stability metric for pattern"""
        pattern_id = self._generate_pattern_id(pattern)
        
        if pattern_id not in self.patterns:
            return 1.0  # New patterns are considered stable
        
        occurrences = self.patterns[pattern_id]['occurrences']
        if occurrences < 2:
            return 1.0  # Not enough data for stability analysis

        # Calculate stability based on recent occurrences
        recent_stability = self.patterns[pattern_id]['stability_history'][-5:]
        
        # Calculate average change in key pattern properties
        strength_changes = []
        coherence_changes = []

        for i in range(len(recent_stability) - 1):
            strength_changes.append(abs(recent_stability[i+1]['strength'] - recent_stability[i]['strength']))
            coherence_changes.append(abs(recent_stability[i+1]['coherence'] - recent_stability[i]['coherence']))

        avg_strength_change = np.mean(strength_changes) if strength_changes else 0
        avg_coherence_change = np.mean(coherence_changes) if coherence_changes else 0

        # Stability is inversely proportional to changes
        stability = 1.0 - (avg_strength_change + avg_coherence_change) / 2
        
        return max(0, min(1, stability))  # Ensure value is within [0, 1]

    def _calculate_pattern_metrics(self, pattern: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Calculate key metrics for pattern"""
        return {
            'strength': pattern.get('strength', 0),
            'coherence': pattern.get('coherence', 0),
            'dimensions': pattern.get('dimensions', []),
            'type': pattern.get('type', '')
        }

    def _update_stability_metrics(self, pattern_id: str):
        """Update overall stability metrics for pattern"""
        if pattern_id in self.patterns:
            pattern_data = self.patterns[pattern_id]
            
            # Calculate average stability
            avg_stability = np.mean([s['strength'] for s in pattern_data['stability_history']])
            
            # Calculate frequency
            frequency = pattern_data['occurrences'] / len(self.evolution_history)
            
            self.stability_metrics[pattern_id] = {
                'avg_stability': avg_stability,
                'frequency': frequency
            }

    def get_stable_patterns(self, threshold: float = 0.7) -> List[Dict]:
        """Retrieve patterns that meet stability threshold"""
        stable_patterns = []
        
        for pattern_id, metrics in self.stability_metrics.items():
            if metrics['avg_stability'] > threshold:
                stable_patterns.append(self.patterns[pattern_id]['pattern'])
        
        return stable_patterns

    def get_frequent_patterns(self, threshold: float = 0.1) -> List[Dict]:
        """Retrieve patterns that meet frequency threshold"""
        frequent_patterns = []
        
        for pattern_id, metrics in self.stability_metrics.items():
            if metrics['frequency'] > threshold:
                frequent_patterns.append(self.patterns[pattern_id]['pattern'])
        
        return frequent_patterns

    def analyze_pattern_evolution(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Analyze pattern evolution over time"""
        evolution_analysis = []
        
        for pattern_id, pattern_data in self.patterns.items():
            evolution = {
                'pattern_id': pattern_id,
                'strength_changes': [],
                'coherence_changes': [],
                'occurrence_timestamps': []
            }
            
            # Find occurrences of this pattern
            for i, event in enumerate(self.evolution_history):
                if event['pattern_id'] == pattern_id:
                    evolution['occurrence_timestamps'].append(event['timestamp'])
                    if len(evolution['strength_changes']) > 0:
                        evolution['strength_changes'].append(
                            event['metrics']['strength'] - evolution['strength_changes'][-1][1]
                        )
                        evolution['coherence_changes'].append(
                            event['metrics']['coherence'] - evolution['coherence_changes'][-1][1]
                        )
                    
                    evolution['strength_changes'].append((event['timestamp'], event['metrics']['strength']))
                    evolution['coherence_changes'].append((event['timestamp'], event['metrics']['coherence']))

            evolution_analysis.append(evolution)

        return evolution_analysis

import networkx as nx

class GraphManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.knowledge_graph = nx.DiGraph()

    def add_node(self, node_id: str, node_type: str, attributes: dict = None):
        """Add a node to the knowledge graph."""
        if attributes is None:
            attributes = {}
        self.knowledge_graph.add_node(node_id, type=node_type, **attributes)

    def add_edge(self, source_node: str, target_node: str, relationship: str, attributes: dict = None):
        """Add an edge between two nodes in the knowledge graph."""
        if attributes is None:
            attributes = {}
        self.knowledge_graph.add_edge(source_node, target_node, relationship=relationship, **attributes)

    def update_node_attributes(self, node_id: str, attributes: dict):
        """Update attributes of a node."""
        if node_id in self.knowledge_graph:
            self.knowledge_graph.nodes[node_id].update(attributes)

    def update_edge_attributes(self, source_node: str, target_node: str, attributes: dict):
        """Update attributes of an edge."""
        if self.knowledge_graph.has_edge(source_node, target_node):
            self.knowledge_graph[source_node][target_node].update(attributes)

    def get_node_attributes(self, node_id: str) -> dict:
        """Get attributes of a node."""
        if node_id in self.knowledge_graph:
            return self.knowledge_graph.nodes[node_id]
        return {}

    def get_edge_attributes(self, source_node: str, target_node: str) -> dict:
        """Get attributes of an edge."""
        if self.knowledge_graph.has_edge(source_node, target_node):
            return self.knowledge_graph[source_node][target_node]
        return {}

    def find_nodes(self, node_type: str = None, attribute_filter: dict = None) -> list:
        """Find nodes based on type and/or attribute filter."""
        nodes = []
        for node_id, attributes in self.knowledge_graph.nodes(data=True):
            if node_type is None or attributes.get('type') == node_type:
                if attribute_filter is None or all(attributes.get(key) == value for key, value in attribute_filter.items()):
                    nodes.append(node_id)
        return nodes

    def find_edges(self, relationship: str = None, attribute_filter: dict = None) -> list:
        """Find edges based on relationship and/or attribute filter."""
        edges = []
        for source, target, attributes in self.knowledge_graph.edges(data=True):
            if relationship is None or attributes.get('relationship') == relationship:
                if attribute_filter is None or all(

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Python

                if attribute_filter is None or all(attributes.get(key) == value for key, value in attribute_filter.items()):
                    edges.append((source, target))
        return edges

    def get_neighbors(self, node_id: str, relationship: str = None) -> list:
        """Get neighbors of a node, optionally filtered by relationship."""
        neighbors = []
        if node_id in self.knowledge_graph:
            for neighbor in self.knowledge_graph.neighbors(node_id):
                if relationship is None or self.knowledge_graph[node_id][neighbor].get('relationship') == relationship:
                    neighbors.append(neighbor)
        return neighbors

    def shortest_path(self, source_node: str, target_node: str) -> list:
        """Find the shortest path between two nodes."""
        try:
            return nx.shortest_path(self.knowledge_graph, source_node, target_node)
        except nx.NetworkXNoPath:
            return []

    def visualize_graph(self):
        """Visualize the knowledge graph (basic implementation)."""
        nx.draw(self.knowledge_graph, with_labels=True)
        
    def get_subgraph(self, node_ids: List[str]) -> nx.DiGraph:
        """
        Extract a subgraph based on a list of node IDs.
        """
        return self.knowledge_graph.subgraph(node_ids)

    def calculate_centrality(self, centrality_type: str = 'degree') -> dict:
        """
        Calculate centrality measures for the nodes in the graph.
        Supported centrality types: 'degree', 'betweenness', 'closeness', 'eigenvector'.
        """
        if centrality_type == 'degree':
            centrality = nx.degree_centrality(self.knowledge_graph)
        elif centrality_type == 'betweenness':
            centrality = nx.betweenness_centrality(self.knowledge_graph)
        elif centrality_type == 'closeness':
            centrality = nx.closeness_centrality(self.knowledge_graph)
        elif centrality_type == 'eigenvector':
            centrality = nx.eigenvector_centrality(self.knowledge_graph)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Centrality type '{centrality_type}' not supported.")
        return centrality
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
      """Return the current state of the graph manager."""
      return {
          'nodes': [{node_id: data} for node_id, data in self.knowledge_graph.nodes(data=True)],
          'edges': [{source: {target: data}} for source, target, data in self.knowledge_graph.edges(data=True)]
      }

import itertools
import numpy as np

class HypothesisGenerator:
    def __init__(self, graph_manager: GraphManager):
        self.graph_manager = graph_manager
        self.hypotheses = []

    def generate_hypotheses(self, pattern_data: list):
        """Generate hypotheses based on observed patterns and the knowledge graph."""
        self.hypotheses = []  # Clear previous hypotheses

        for pattern in pattern_data:
            if pattern['type'] == 'temporal_pattern':
                self._generate_temporal_hypotheses(pattern)
            elif pattern['type'] == 'spatial_pattern':
                self._generate_spatial_hypotheses(pattern)
            elif pattern['type'] == 'causal_pattern':
                self._generate_causal_hypotheses(pattern)
            # Add more pattern types as needed

        return self.hypotheses

    def _generate_temporal_hypotheses(self, pattern: dict):
        """Generate hypotheses based on temporal patterns."""
        dimensions = pattern['dimensions']
        sequence = pattern['sequence']

        # Example hypothesis: Predict next pattern in sequence
        if len(sequence) >= 2:
            last_pattern = sequence[-1]
            predicted_dimensions = self._predict_next_dimensions(dimensions)
            
            self.hypotheses.append({
                'type': 'temporal_prediction',
                'basis': pattern,
                'prediction': f"Pattern of type {last_pattern[0].get('type', 'unknown')} involving dimensions {predicted_dimensions} will occur next.",
                'confidence': self._calculate_temporal_confidence(sequence),
                'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
            })

    def _predict_next_dimensions(self, dimensions: list) -> list:
      """Predict the next dimensions involved in a pattern based on historical data."""
      # This is a placeholder for a more sophisticated prediction mechanism
      # You might want to use machine learning techniques here
      
      # Simple approach: choose the next dimension in a cyclic manner
      last_dimension = dimensions[-1]
      next_dimension = (last_dimension + 1) % (max(dimensions) + 1)  # Assuming dimensions are integers
      return [next_dimension]

    def _calculate_temporal_confidence(self, sequence: list) -> float:
      """Calculate confidence based on the consistency and frequency of the temporal sequence."""
      if not sequence:
          return 0.0

      # Consistency check: see if the sequence has a repeating pattern
      is_consistent = len(set(map(tuple, sequence))) <= len(sequence) / 2 

      # Frequency check: more frequent sequences get higher confidence
      frequency_score = min(1.0, len(sequence) / 10.0)  # Normalize by an arbitrary factor

      # Combine consistency and frequency into a confidence score
      confidence = frequency_score * (1 if is_consistent else 0.5)  # Reduce confidence if not consistent

      return confidence

    def _generate_spatial_hypotheses(self, pattern: dict):
        """Generate hypotheses based on spatial patterns."""
        dimensions = pattern['dimensions']

        # Example hypothesis: Infer relationships between dimensions
        for dim_pair in itertools.combinations(dimensions, 2):
            node1, node2 = f"dim_{dim_pair[0]}", f"dim_{dim_pair[1]}"

            # Check if nodes exist in the knowledge graph
            if self.graph_manager.knowledge_graph.has_node(node1) and self.graph_manager.knowledge_graph.has_node(node2):
                
                # Infer relationship based on graph proximity
                try:
                    path_length = nx.shortest_path_length(self.graph_manager.knowledge_graph, node1, node2)
                    relationship_strength = 1 / path_length if path_length > 0 else 1
                    
                    self.hypotheses.append({
                        'type': 'spatial_inference',
                        'basis': pattern,
                        'inference': f"Dimensions {dim_pair[0]} and {dim_pair[1]} are related.",
                        'confidence': relationship_strength,
                        'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
                    })

                except nx.NetworkXNoPath:
                    pass
                
    def _generate_causal_hypotheses(self, pattern: dict):
        """Generate hypotheses based on causal patterns."""
        cause = pattern['cause']
        effect = pattern['effect']
        confidence = pattern['confidence']

        # Example hypothesis: Infer causal relationship between cause and effect
        self.hypotheses.append({
            'type': 'causal_inference',
            'basis': pattern,
            'inference': f"Pattern {cause} causes {effect}.",
            'confidence': confidence,
            'timestamp': np.datetime64('now')
        })

    def refine_hypotheses(self, new_data: list):
        """Refine existing hypotheses based on new data."""
        updated_hypotheses = []
        for hypothesis in self.hypotheses:
            if hypothesis['type'] == 'temporal_prediction':
                # Example: Check if prediction matches new data
                predicted_pattern_type = hypothesis['prediction'].split(" ")[2]
                predicted_dimensions = [int(d) for d in hypothesis['prediction'].split(" ")[5].split(",")]

                for pattern in new_data:
                    if pattern['type'] == predicted_pattern_type and set(pattern['dimensions']) == set(predicted_dimensions):
                        hypothesis['confidence'] = min(1.0, hypothesis['confidence'] + 0.2)  # Increase confidence
                        break
                else:
                    hypothesis['confidence'] = max(0.0, hypothesis['confidence'] - 0.1)  # Decrease confidence

                updated_hypotheses.append(hypothesis)
            
            elif hypothesis['type'] == 'spatial_inference':
              # Example: Check if spatial inference is consistent with new data
              related_dimensions = [int(dim) for dim in hypothesis['inference'].split(" ")[1].split(",")]

              # Look for new patterns involving these dimensions
              for pattern in new_data:
                  if 'dimensions' in pattern and set(related_dimensions).issubset(set(pattern['dimensions'])):
                      # Increase confidence if new pattern confirms the inference
                      hypothesis['confidence'] = min(1.0, hypothesis['confidence'] + 0.1)
                      break
                  else:
                     hypothesis['confidence'] = max(0.0, hypothesis['confidence'] - 0.05)

              updated_hypotheses.append(hypothesis)

            elif hypothesis['type'] == 'causal_inference':
              # Example: Check if causal inference holds true in new data
              cause_pattern_type = hypothesis['basis']['cause']['type']
              effect_pattern_type = hypothesis['basis']['effect']['type']

              # Look for new instances of cause and effect patterns
              for i in range(len(new_data) - 1):
                  cause_pattern = new_data[i]
                  effect_pattern = new_data[i+1]

                  if cause_pattern['type'] == cause_pattern_type and effect_pattern['type'] == effect_pattern_type:
                      # Increase confidence if cause is followed by effect
                      hypothesis['confidence'] = min(1.0, hypothesis['confidence'] + 0.1)
                      break
                  else:
                      hypothesis['confidence'] = max(0.0, hypothesis['confidence'] - 0.05)
              updated_hypotheses.append(hypothesis)
            else:
              updated_hypotheses.append(hypothesis) # Add hypothesis to updated list if no conditions are met

        self.hypotheses = updated_hypotheses
        
    def get_top_hypotheses(self, n: int = 5) -> list:
        """Get the top n hypotheses with the highest confidence."""
        return sorted(self.hypotheses, key=lambda x: x['confidence'], reverse=True)[:n]

    def validate_hypotheses(self, external_data) -> dict:
      """
      Validate the generated hypotheses against an external dataset.
      
      Args:
          external_data: Data from an external source used for validation.
          
      Returns:
          A dictionary containing validation results for each hypothesis.
      """
      validation_results = {}
      for hypothesis in self.hypotheses:
          validation_result = self._validate_hypothesis(hypothesis, external_data)
          validation_results[hypothesis['type'] + str(hypothesis['basis'])] = validation_result
          
      return validation_results
    
    def _validate_hypothesis(self, hypothesis: dict, external_data) -> dict:
      """
      Validate a single hypothesis against external data.
      """
      if hypothesis['type'] == 'temporal_prediction':
          return self._validate_temporal_prediction(hypothesis, external_data)
      elif hypothesis['type'] == 'spatial_inference':
          return self._validate_spatial_inference(hypothesis, external_data)
      elif hypothesis['type'] == 'causal_inference':
          return self._validate_causal_inference(hypothesis, external_data)
      else:
          return {'valid': False, 'reason': 'Unknown hypothesis type'}
      
    def _validate_temporal_prediction(self, hypothesis: dict, external_data) -> dict:
        """
        Validate a temporal prediction against external data.
        """
        # Extract the predicted pattern type and dimensions from the hypothesis
        predicted_pattern_type = hypothesis['prediction'].split(" ")[2]
        predicted_dimensions = [int(d) for d in hypothesis['prediction'].split(" ")[5].split(",")]

        # Check if the predicted pattern occurs in the external data
        for pattern in external_data:
            if pattern['type'] == predicted_pattern_type and set(pattern['dimensions']) == set(predicted_dimensions):
                return {'valid': True, 'reason': 'Temporal prediction matched in external data'}

        return {'valid': False, 'reason': 'Temporal prediction not found in external data'}

    def _validate_spatial_inference(self, hypothesis: dict, external_data) -> dict:
        """
        Validate a spatial inference against external data.
        """
        # Extract the related dimensions from the hypothesis
        related_dimensions = [int(dim) for dim in hypothesis['inference'].split(" ")[1].split(",")]

        # Check if the spatial relationship is confirmed in the external data
        for pattern in external_data:
            if 'dimensions' in pattern and set(related_dimensions).issubset(set(pattern['dimensions'])):
                return {'valid': True, 'reason': 'Spatial inference confirmed in external data'}

        return {'valid': False, 'reason': 'Spatial inference not confirmed in external data'}

    def _validate_causal_inference(self, hypothesis: dict, external_data) -> dict:
        """
        Validate a causal inference against external data.
        """
        # Extract the cause and effect pattern types from the hypothesis
        cause_pattern_type = hypothesis['basis']['cause']['type']
        effect_pattern_type = hypothesis['basis']['effect']['type']

        # Check if the causal relationship holds in the external data
        for i in range(len(external_data) - 1):
            cause_pattern = external_data[i]
            effect_pattern = external_data[i+1]

            if cause_pattern['type'] == cause_pattern_type and effect_pattern['type'] == effect_pattern_type:
                return {'valid': True, 'reason': 'Causal inference confirmed in external data'}

        return {'valid': False, 'reason': 'Causal inference not confirmed in external data'}
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the hypothesis generator."""
        return {
            'hypotheses': self.hypotheses,
            'top_hypotheses': self.get_top_hypotheses()
        }

import numpy as np

class ScenarioGenerator:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int, num_scenarios: int = 10):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.num_scenarios = num_scenarios
        self.scenarios = []

    def generate_scenarios(self, initial_conditions: dict, steps: int) -> list:
        """Generate multiple scenarios based on initial conditions and simulation steps."""
        self.scenarios = []  # Clear previous scenarios

        for _ in range(self.num_scenarios):
            scenario = self._generate_single_scenario(initial_conditions, steps)
            self.scenarios.append(scenario)

        return self.scenarios

    def _generate_single_scenario(self, initial_conditions: dict, steps: int) -> list:
      """Generate a single simulation scenario."""
      scenario = [initial_conditions]
      current_state = initial_conditions.copy()

      for _ in range(steps):
          next_state = self._calculate_next_state(current_state)
          scenario.append(next_state)
          current_state = next_state

      return scenario

    def _calculate_next_state(self, current_state: dict) -> dict:
        """Calculate the next state based on the current state."""
        next_state = current_state.copy()

        # Example: Simple stochastic evolution of dimensions
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            if f"dim_{dim}_value" in next_state:
                # Apply a random walk with a tendency to return to the initial value
                random_change = np.random.normal(0, 0.1)  # Gaussian noise
                tendency = 0.05 * (initial_conditions[f"dim_{dim}_value"] - next_state[f"dim_{dim}_value"])
                next_state[f"dim_{dim}_value"] += random_change + tendency

        return next_state

    def analyze_scenarios(self) -> dict:
        """Analyze generated scenarios to find common patterns or divergent outcomes."""
        if not self.scenarios:
            return {}

        # Example analysis: Calculate average and standard deviation for each dimension at each step
        analysis = {}
        for step in range(len(self.scenarios[0])):
            step_data = {f"dim_{dim}_avg": [] for dim in range(self.dimensions)}
            for scenario in self.scenarios:
                for dim in range(self.dimensions):
                    if f"dim_{dim}_value" in scenario[step]:
                        step_data[f"dim_{dim}_avg"].append(scenario[step][f"dim_{dim}_value"])

            for dim in range(self.dimensions):
                if step_data[f"dim_{dim}_avg"]:
                    analysis.setdefault(f"dim_{dim}_stats", []).append

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Python

                    analysis.setdefault(f"dim_{dim}_stats", []).append({
                        'step': step,
                        'avg': np.mean(step_data[f"dim_{dim}_avg"]),
                        'std': np.std(step_data[f"dim_{dim}_avg"])
                    })

        return analysis

    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the scenario generator."""
        return {
            'dimensions': self.dimensions,
            'num_scenarios': self.num_scenarios,
            'scenarios': self.scenarios,
            'analysis': self.analyze_scenarios()
        }

perspective_engine/simulation/pattern_validator.py

class PatternValidator:
    def __init__(self, resonance_processor: AdvancedResonanceProcessor):
        self.resonance_processor = resonance_processor
        self.validation_results = []

    def validate_patterns(self, patterns: list, simulation_data: list) -> list:
        """Validate detected patterns against simulation data."""
        self.validation_results = []  # Clear previous results

        for pattern in patterns:
            result = self._validate_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
            self.validation_results.append(result)

        return self.validation_results

    def _validate_pattern(self, pattern: dict, simulation_data: list) -> dict:
        """Validate a single pattern against simulation data."""
        pattern_type = pattern['type']
        dimensions = pattern.get('dimensions', [])

        if pattern_type == 'temporal_pattern':
            return self._validate_temporal_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        elif pattern_type == 'spatial_pattern':
            return self._validate_spatial_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        elif pattern_type == 'causal_pattern':
            return self._validate_causal_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        elif pattern_type == 'advanced_resonance_pattern':
            return self._validate_advanced_resonance_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        else:
            return {
                'pattern': pattern,
                'valid': False,
                'reason': 'Unknown pattern type'
            }

    def _validate_temporal_pattern(self, pattern: dict, simulation_data: list) -> dict:
        """Validate a temporal pattern against simulation data."""
        sequence = pattern['sequence']
        
        # Check if the temporal sequence appears in the simulation data
        for i in range(len(simulation_data) - len(sequence) + 1):
            match = True
            for j, seq_pattern in enumerate(sequence):
                if not self._compare_patterns(seq_pattern, simulation_data[i + j]):
                    match = False
                    break
            if match:
                return {
                    'pattern': pattern,
                    'valid': True,
                    'reason': 'Temporal sequence found in simulation data'
                }

        return {
            'pattern': pattern,
            'valid': False,
            'reason': 'Temporal sequence not found in simulation data'
        }

    def _validate_spatial_pattern(self, pattern: dict, simulation_data: list) -> dict:
        """Validate a spatial pattern against simulation data."""
        dimensions = pattern['dimensions']

        # Check if the spatial pattern (dimensions involved) is present in the simulation data
        for data_point in simulation_data:
            if set(dimensions).issubset(set(data_point.get('dimensions', []))):
                return {
                    'pattern': pattern,
                    'valid': True,
                    'reason': 'Spatial pattern found in simulation data'
                }

        return {
            'pattern': pattern,
            'valid': False,
            'reason': 'Spatial pattern not found in simulation data'
        }
    
    def _validate_causal_pattern(self, pattern: dict, simulation_data: list) -> dict:
        """Validate a causal pattern against simulation data."""
        cause_type = pattern['cause']['type']
        effect_type = pattern['effect']['type']

        # Check if the cause-effect relationship holds in the simulation data
        for i in range(len(simulation_data) - 1):
            if simulation_data[i]['type'] == cause_type and simulation_data[i + 1]['type'] == effect_type:
                return {
                    'pattern': pattern,
                    'valid': True,
                    'reason': 'Causal relationship found in simulation data'
                }

        return {
            'pattern': pattern,
            'valid': False,
            'reason': 'Causal relationship not found in simulation data'
        }

    def _validate_advanced_resonance_pattern(self, pattern: dict, simulation_data: list) -> dict:
        """Validate an advanced resonance pattern against simulation data."""
        # Check for the presence of all component patterns in the simulation data
        temporal_valid = self._validate_temporal_pattern(pattern['temporal_component'], simulation_data)
        spatial_valid = self._validate_spatial_pattern(pattern['spatial_component'], simulation_data)
        causal_valid = self._validate_causal_pattern(pattern['causal_component'], simulation_data)
        
        if temporal_valid['valid'] and spatial_valid['valid'] and causal_valid['valid']:
          return {
              'pattern': pattern,
              'valid': True,
              'reason': 'All component patterns found in simulation data'
          }

        return {
            'pattern': pattern,
            'valid': False,
            'reason': 'Not all component patterns found in simulation data'
        }

    def _compare_patterns(self, pattern1: dict, pattern2: dict) -> bool:
        """Compare two patterns for similarity."""
        # Implement logic to compare two patterns based on their attributes
        # This is a placeholder, and you should define your own comparison logic
        return pattern1['type'] == pattern2['type']

    def get_validation_summary(self) -> dict:
        """Provide a summary of pattern validation results."""
        summary = {
            'total_patterns': len(self.validation_results),
            'valid_patterns': sum(1 for r in self.validation_results if r['valid']),
            'invalid_patterns': sum(1 for r in self.validation_results if not r['valid'])
        }
        return summary
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the pattern validator."""
        return {
            'validation_results': self.validation_results,
            'validation_summary': self.get_validation_summary()
        }

perspective_engine/core/graph_engine.py

import networkx as nx
import numpy as np

class GraphEngine:
    def __init__(self, resonance_processor: AdvancedResonanceProcessor):
        self.resonance_processor = resonance_processor
        self.graph = nx.Graph()
        self.node_positions = {}  # Store positions for visualization

    def update_graph(self, patterns: list):
        """Update the graph based on detected patterns."""
        self.graph.clear()  # Clear the existing graph

        for pattern in patterns:
            self._add_pattern_to_graph(pattern)

    def _add_pattern_to_graph(self, pattern: dict):
        """Add a pattern to the graph."""
        pattern_id = self.resonance_processor.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern)
        
        # Add pattern as a node
        self.graph.add_node(pattern_id, type=pattern['type'], timestamp=pattern['timestamp'])
        
        if 'dimensions' in pattern:
            for dim in pattern['dimensions']:
                dim_node = f"dim_{dim}"
                
                # Add dimension as a node if it doesn't exist
                if not self.graph.has_node(dim_node):
                    self.graph.add_node(dim_node, type='dimension')
                    
                # Connect pattern to its dimensions
                self.graph.add_edge(pattern_id, dim_node)

        # Connect patterns based on relationships
        if pattern['type'] == 'causal_pattern':
            cause_id = self.resonance_processor.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern['cause'])
            effect_id = self.resonance_processor.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern['effect'])
            self.graph.add_edge(cause_id, effect_id, type='causal')

    def analyze_graph(self) -> dict:
        """Analyze the graph to identify key structures and metrics."""
        analysis = {}

        # Basic metrics
        analysis['num_nodes'] = self.graph.number_of_nodes()
        analysis['num_edges'] = self.graph.number_of_edges()
        analysis['avg_degree'] = np.mean([d for _, d in self.graph.degree()]) if self.graph.number_of_nodes() > 0 else 0

        # Centrality measures
        analysis['degree_centrality'] = nx.degree_centrality(self.graph)
        analysis['betweenness_centrality'] = nx.betweenness_centrality(self.graph)
        analysis['closeness_centrality'] = nx.closeness_centrality(self.graph)
        analysis['eigenvector_centrality'] = nx.eigenvector_centrality_numpy(self.graph)

        # Community detection
        communities = nx.community.greedy_modularity_communities(self.graph)
        analysis['num_communities'] = len(communities)
        analysis['communities'] = communities

        return analysis
    
    def visualize_graph(self, layout: str = 'spring', save_to_file: bool = False, filename: str = 'graph.png'):
        """
        Visualize the graph using different layout algorithms.
        """
        if layout == 'spring':
            pos = nx.spring_layout(self.graph, seed=42)  # Using a seed for reproducibility
        elif layout == 'spectral':
            pos = nx.spectral_layout(self.graph)
        elif layout == 'kamada_kawai':
            pos = nx.kamada_kawai_layout(self.graph)
        elif layout == 'circular':
            pos = nx.circular_layout(self.graph)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Layout '{layout}' not recognized.")

        # Use stored positions if available, otherwise calculate new positions
        for node in self.graph.nodes():
            if node not in self.node_positions:
                self.node_positions[node] = pos[node]

        # Draw the graph
        nx.draw(self.graph, self.node_positions, with_labels=True, node_color='skyblue', node_size=800, edge_color='gray', font_size=10, font_weight='bold')

        # Save to file or show the plot
        if save_to_file:
            plt.savefig(filename)
        else:
            plt.show()

    def get_pattern_subgraph(self, pattern_type: str) -> nx.Graph:
        """
        Extract a subgraph containing only nodes of a specific pattern type.
        """
        nodes = [node for node, data in self.graph.nodes(data=True) if data.get('type') == pattern_type]
        return self.graph.subgraph(nodes)

    def get_dimension_subgraph(self, dimension: int) -> nx.Graph:
        """
        Extract a subgraph containing only nodes related to a specific dimension.
        """
        dim_node = f"dim_{dimension}"
        if not self.graph.has_node(dim_node):
            return nx.Graph()  # Return an empty graph if the dimension node doesn't exist

        nodes = [node for node in self.graph.nodes() if self.graph.has_edge(node, dim_node) or node == dim_node]
        return self.graph.subgraph(nodes)
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the graph engine."""
        return {
            'graph': {
                'nodes': list(self.graph.nodes(data=True)),
                'edges': list(self.graph.edges(data=True))
            },
            'analysis': self.analyze_graph()
        }

import numpy as np

class PatternAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, pattern_registry: AdvancedPatternRegistry):
        self.pattern_registry = pattern_registry

    def analyze_patterns(self) -> dict:
        """Analyze patterns from the registry for insights and trends."""
        analysis = {}

        # Basic pattern statistics
        analysis['total_patterns'] = len(self.pattern_registry.patterns)
        analysis['pattern_types'] = self._get_pattern_type_distribution()

        # Stability and frequency analysis
        stable_patterns = self.pattern_registry.get_stable_patterns()
        frequent_patterns = self.pattern_registry.get_frequent_patterns()
        analysis['stable_patterns'] = [self._summarize_pattern(p) for p in stable_patterns]
        analysis['frequent_patterns'] = [self._summarize_pattern(p) for p in frequent_patterns]

        # Evolution analysis
        analysis['pattern_evolution'] = self.pattern_registry.analyze_pattern_evolution()

        # Correlation analysis
        analysis['correlations'] = self._calculate_correlations(self.pattern_registry.patterns.values())

        return analysis

    def _get_pattern_type_distribution(self) -> dict:
        """Calculate the distribution of different pattern types."""
        distribution = {}
        for pattern_data in self.pattern_registry.patterns.values():
            pattern_type = pattern_data['pattern']['type']
            distribution[pattern_type] = distribution.get(pattern_type, 0) + 1
        return distribution

    def _summarize_pattern(self, pattern: dict) -> dict:
        """Summarize key information about a pattern."""
        return {
            'id': self.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern),
            'type': pattern['type'],
            'dimensions': pattern.get('dimensions', []),
            'strength': pattern.get('strength', 0),
            'coherence': pattern.get('coherence', 0),
            'timestamp': pattern.get('timestamp', str(np.datetime64('NaT'))),
            # Add more fields as needed
        }
    
    def _calculate_correlations(self, patterns) -> dict:
        """Calculate correlations between different pattern features."""
        correlations = {}

        # Extract relevant features for correlation analysis
        features = {
            'strength': [],
            'coherence': [],
            'num_dimensions': [],
            # Add more features as needed
        }
        for pattern_data in patterns:
            pattern = pattern_data['pattern']
            features['strength'].append(pattern.get('strength', 0))
            features['coherence'].append(pattern.get('coherence', 0))
            features['num_dimensions'].append(len(pattern.get('dimensions', [])))
            # Add more features as needed

        # Calculate correlations
        for feature1 in features:
            for feature2 in features:
                if feature1 != feature2:
                    correlation = np.corrcoef(features[feature1], features[feature2])[0, 1]
                    correlations[f"{feature1}_vs_{feature2}"] = correlation

        return correlations
    
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the pattern analyzer."""
        return {
            'analysis': self.analyze_patterns()
        }

import uuid
import time

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, capacity: float, role: str):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.capacity = capacity  # Processing capacity
        self.role = role # i.e.: "kaleidoscope_core", "perspective_engine", "data_processing", "system_monitoring"
        self.status = "idle"  # Current status (e.g., "idle", "processing", "standby", "failed")
        self.last_heartbeat = time.time()
        self.tasks = []

    def assign_task(self, task: dict):
        """Assign a task to the node."""
        self.tasks.append(task)
        self.status = "processing"

    def update_status(self, status: str):
        """Update the node's status."""
        self.status = status

    def send_heartbeat(self):
        """Send a heartbeat signal to indicate the node is alive."""
        self.last_heartbeat = time.time()

    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the node."""
        return {
            'node_id': self.node_id,
            'capacity': self.capacity,
            'role': self.role,
            'status': self.status,
            'last_heartbeat': self.last_heartbeat,
            'tasks': self.tasks
        }

import uuid
import time

class Node:
    def __init__(self, node_id: str, capacity: float, role: str, ip_address: str, port: int):
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.capacity = capacity  # Processing capacity
        self.role = role # i.e.: "kaleidoscope_core", "perspective_engine", "data_processing", "system_monitoring"
        self.status = "idle"  # Current status (e.g., "idle", "processing", "standby", "failed")
        self.last_heartbeat = time.time()
        self.tasks = []
        self.ip_address = ip_address
        self.port = port

    def assign_task(self, task: dict):
        """Assign a task to the node."""
        self.tasks.append(task)
        self.status = "processing"

    def update_status(self, status: str):
        """Update the node's status."""
        self.status = status

    def send_heartbeat(self):
        """Send a heartbeat signal to indicate the node is alive."""
        self.last_heartbeat = time.time()

    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the node."""
        return {
            'node_id': self.node_id,
            'capacity': self.capacity,
            'role': self.role,
            'status': self.status,
            'last_heartbeat': self.last_heartbeat,
            'tasks': self.tasks,
            'ip_address': self.ip_address,
            'port': self.port
        }

class NodeLifeCycleManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes = {}
        self.failed_nodes = {}

    def register_node(self, capacity: float, role: str, ip_address: str, port: int) -> str:
        """Register a new node with the system."""
        node_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        new_node = Node(node_id, capacity, role, ip_address, port)
        self.nodes[node_id] = new_node
        return node_id

    def unregister_node(self, node_id: str):
        """Unregister a node from the system."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            del self.nodes[node_id]

    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Node:
        """Retrieve a node by its ID."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
          return self.nodes[node_id]
        else:
          return None

    def update_node_status(self, node_id: str, status: str):
        """Update the status of a node."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node_id].update_status(status)

    def monitor_nodes(self, heartbeat_interval: int = 10, failure_threshold: int = 30):
        """Monitor nodes and detect failures based on heartbeat."""
        current_time = time.time()
        for node_id, node in list(self.nodes.items()):
            if current_time - node.last_heartbeat > failure_threshold:
                print(f"Node {node_id} has not sent a heartbeat within the threshold. Marking as failed.")
                self.failed_nodes[node_id] = self.nodes.pop(node_id)
                self.failed_nodes[node_id].update_status("failed")

    def get_all_nodes(self, status_filter: str = None) -> list:
        """Retrieve all nodes, optionally filtered by status."""
        if status_filter:
            return [node.get_state() for node in self.nodes.values() if node.status == status_filter]
        else:
            return [node.get_state() for node in self.nodes.values()]

    def get_failed_nodes(self) -> list:
        """Retrieve all failed nodes."""
        return [node.get_state() for node in self.failed_nodes.values()]

    def assign_task_to_node(self, node_id: str, task: dict):
        """Assign a task to a specific node."""
        if node_id in self.nodes:
            self.nodes[node_id].assign_task(task)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Node with ID {node_id} not found.")
            
    def reassign_tasks(self, failed_node_id: str):
      """
      Reassign tasks from a failed node to other available nodes.
      """
      if failed_node_id in self.failed_nodes:
          failed_node = self.failed_nodes[failed_node_id]
          tasks = failed_node.tasks

          # Find available nodes with the same role
          available_nodes = [node for node in self.nodes.values() if node.role == failed_node.role and node.status == "idle"]

          if available_nodes:
              # Distribute tasks among available nodes
              for i, task in enumerate(tasks):
                  node_index = i % len(available_nodes)  # Cycle through available nodes
                  available_nodes[node_index].assign_task(task)
                  print(f"Task reassigned from failed node {failed_node_id} to node {available_nodes[node_index].node_id}")
          else:
              print(f"No available nodes with the same role to reassign tasks from failed node {failed_node_id}")

          # Clear the tasks list of the failed node
          failed_node.tasks = []
          
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the node life cycle manager."""
        return {
            'nodes': {node_id: node.get_state() for node_id, node in self.nodes.items()},
            'failed_nodes': {node_id: node.get_state() for node_id, node in self.failed_nodes.items()}
        }

class EnergyManager:
    def __init__(self, node_manager: NodeLifeCycleManager):
        self.node_manager = node_manager
        self.total_energy_capacity = self._calculate_total_energy_capacity()

    def _calculate_total_energy_capacity(self) -> float:
        """Calculate the total energy capacity of all nodes."""
        return sum(node.capacity for node in self.node_manager.nodes.values())

    def get_energy_distribution(self) -> dict:
        """Get the current energy distribution across all nodes."""
        distribution = {}
        for node_id, node in self.node_manager.nodes.items():
            distribution[node_id] = {
                'capacity': node.capacity,
                'current_load': self._calculate_node_load(node),
                'status': node.status
            }
        return distribution

    def _calculate_node_load(self, node: Node) -> float:
        """Calculate the current load of a node based on its tasks."""
        # Placeholder for a more sophisticated load calculation
        return len(node.tasks) * 0.1  # Assume each task consumes 10% of capacity

    def optimize_energy_usage(self, threshold: float = 0.8):
        """Optimize energy usage across nodes."""
        for node_id, node in self.node_manager.nodes.items():
            load = self._calculate_node_load(node)
            if load > threshold * node.capacity:
                self._redistribute_load(node)

    def _redistribute_load(self, overloaded_node: Node):
        """Redistribute tasks from an overloaded node to underloaded nodes."""
        underloaded_nodes = [
            n for n in self.node_manager.nodes.values()
            if n.node_id != overloaded_node.node_id and self._calculate_node_load(n) < 0.5 * n.capacity
        ]

        if underloaded_nodes:
            for task in overloaded_node.tasks:
                target_node = underloaded_nodes.pop(0)
                target_node.assign_task(task)
                overloaded_node.tasks.remove(task)
                if not underloaded_nodes:
                    break

    def get_low_energy_nodes(self, threshold: float = 0.2) -> list:
        """Identify nodes that are low on energy."""
        low_energy_nodes = []
        for node_id, node in self.node_manager.nodes.items():
            if self._calculate_node_load(node) < threshold * node.capacity:
                low_energy_nodes.append(node_id)
        return low_energy_nodes

    def set_node_energy_saving_mode(self, node_id: str, enabled: bool):
        """Enable or disable energy saving mode for a node."""
        node = self.node_manager.get_node(node_id)
        if node:
            if enabled:
                # Reduce task load to conserve energy
                while self._calculate_node_load(node) > 0.3 * node.capacity and node.tasks:
                    node.tasks.pop()
                node.status = "energy_saving"
            else:
                node.status = "idle"  # Return to normal operation
                
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Return the current state of the energy manager."""
        return {
            'total_energy_capacity': self.total_energy_capacity,
            'energy_distribution': self.get_energy_distribution()
        }

import random

class TaskDistributor:
    def __init__(self, node_manager: NodeLifeCycleManager):
        self.node_manager = node_manager

    def distribute_tasks(self, tasks: list):
        """Distribute tasks among available nodes based on their roles and capacity."""
        for task in tasks:
            eligible_nodes = self._find_eligible_nodes(task)
            if eligible_nodes:
                chosen_node = self._choose_node(eligible_nodes)
                chosen_node.assign_task(task)
            else:
                print(f"No eligible node found for task: {task['task_id']}")

    def _find_eligible_nodes(self, task: dict) -> list:
        """Find nodes that are eligible to execute a given task."""
        eligible_nodes = []
        for node in self.node_manager.nodes.values():
            if node.status == "idle" and node.role == task["required_role"]:
                load = len(node.tasks) / node.capacity if node.capacity > 0 else 0
                if load < 0.8:  # Consider nodes below 80% load
                  eligible_nodes.append(node)
        return eligible_nodes

    def _choose_node(self, eligible_nodes: list) -> Node:
        """Choose a node from the list of eligible nodes."""
        # Simple strategy: choose the node with the least load
        return min(eligible_nodes, key=lambda node: len(node.tasks))
    
    def rebalance_tasks(self):
      """
      Rebalance tasks across nodes to ensure even distribution and optimal performance.
      """
      # Calculate the average load across nodes
      total_load = sum(len(node.tasks) for node in self.node_manager.nodes.values())
      num_nodes = len(self.node_manager.nodes)
      average_load = total_load / num_nodes if num_nodes > 0 else 0

      underloaded_nodes = []
      overloaded_nodes = []

      for node in self.node_manager.nodes.values():
          if len(node.tasks) < average_load * 0.8:  # 20% below average
              underloaded_nodes.append(node)
          elif len(node.tasks) > average_load * 1.2:  # 20% above average
              overloaded_nodes.append(node)

      # Redistribute tasks from overloaded to underloaded nodes
      for overloaded_node in overloaded_nodes:
          while len(overloaded_node.tasks) > average_load and underloaded_nodes:
              task = overloaded_node.tasks.pop()
              target_node = underloaded_nodes.pop(0)
              target_node.assign_task(task)
              print(f"Task {task['task_id']} reassigned from node {overloaded_node.node_id} to node {target_node.node_id}")

              # Check if the target node is no longer underloaded
              if len(target_node.tasks) >= average_load * 0.8:
                  # If not, put it back in the list for potential reuse
                  underloaded_nodes.insert(0, target_node)
                  
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the task distributor."""
        return {
            'nodes': {node_id: {'tasks': node.tasks, 'load': len(node.tasks) / node.capacity if node.capacity > 0 else 0} for node_id, node in self.node_manager.nodes.items()}
        }

node_management/failsafe/failure_detection.py
Python

import time

class FailureDetector:
    def __init__(self, node_manager: NodeLifeCycleManager, heartbeat_interval: int = 10, failure_threshold: int = 30):
        self.node_manager = node_manager
        self.heartbeat_interval = heartbeat_interval
        self.failure_threshold = failure_threshold
        self.last_check = time.time()

    def detect_failures(self):
        """Detect node failures based on heartbeat."""
        current_time = time.time()
        if current_time - self.last_check >= self.heartbeat_interval:
            for node_id, node in list(self.node_manager.nodes.items()):
                if current_time - node.last_heartbeat > self.failure_threshold:
                    print(f"Node {node_id} has not sent a heartbeat within the threshold. Marking as failed.")
                    self.node_manager.failed_nodes[node_id] = self.node_manager.nodes.pop(node_id)
                    self.node_manager.failed_nodes[node_id].update_status("failed")
            self.last_check = current_time
            
    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the failure detector."""
        return {
            'heartbeat_interval': self.heartbeat_interval,
            'failure_threshold': self.failure_threshold,
            'last_check': self.last_check
        }

node_management/failsafe/recovery_mechanisms.py
Python

class RecoveryMechanisms:
    def __init__(self, node_manager: NodeLifeCycleManager, task_distributor: TaskDistributor):
        self.node_manager = node_manager
        self.task_distributor = task_distributor

    def recover_node(self, node_id: str) -> bool:
        """Attempt to recover a failed node."""
        if node_id in self.node_manager.failed_nodes:
            failed_node = self.node_manager.failed_nodes[node_id]
            
            # Attempt to re-register the node
            try:
                new_node_id = self.node_manager.register_node(failed_node.capacity, failed_node.role, failed_node.ip_address, failed_node.port)
                print(f"Node {node_id} recovered and re-registered with new ID {new_node_id}.")
                
                # Reassign tasks that were assigned to the failed node
                self.task_distributor.reassign_tasks(node_id)

                # Remove the node from the failed nodes list
                del self.node_manager.failed_nodes[node_id]
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Failed to recover node {node_id}: {e}")
                return False
        else:
            print(f"Node {node_id} is not in the list of failed nodes.")
            return False

    def get_state(self) -> dict:
        """Returns the current state of the recovery mechanisms."""
        return {
            'failed_nodes': list(self.node_manager.failed_nodes.keys())
        }
