import unittest
import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.perspective_processor import PerspectiveProcessor, PerspectiveState

class TestPerspectiveProcessor(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.dimensions = 4
        self.processor = PerspectiveProcessor(dimensions=self.dimensions)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.processor.dimensions, self.dimensions)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.processor.state.graph.nodes), self.dimensions)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.processor.state, PerspectiveState))

    def test_process_perspective(self):
        input_data = np.random.rand(self.dimensions, 4) # 4 is the dimensionality of the data after applying quaternion rotation
        result = self.processor.process_perspective(input_data)
        self.assertIn('patterns', result)
        self.assertIn('perspectives', result)
        self.assertIn('state', result)
        self.assertTrue(len(result['patterns']) > 0)

    def test_apply_dimensional_transform(self):
        data = np.random.rand(self.dimensions, 4)
        transformed_data = self.processor._apply_dimensional_transform(data)
        self.assertEqual(transformed_data.shape, data.shape)
        self.assertTrue(transformed_data.dtype == np.complex128)

    def test_generate_resonance_patterns(self):
        data = np.random.rand(self.dimensions, 360) + 1j * np.random.rand(self.dimensions, 360)
        patterns = self.processor._generate_resonance_patterns(data)
        self.assertTrue(len(patterns) > 0)
        for pattern in patterns:
            self.assertIn('dimension', pattern)
            self.assertIn('position', pattern)
            self.assertIn('strength', pattern)
            self.assertIn('phase', pattern)

    def test_calculate_resonance_field(self):
        data = np.random.rand(4)
        field = self.processor._calculate_resonance_field(data)
        self.assertEqual(field.shape, (360,))
        self.assertTrue(field.dtype == np.complex128)

    def test_detect_field_patterns(self):
        field = np.zeros(360, dtype=np.complex128)
        field[180] = 1 + 0j  # Strong peak at 180 degrees
        patterns = self.processor._detect_field_patterns(field, 0)
        self.assertTrue(len(patterns) == 1)
        self.assertEqual(patterns[0]['position'], 180)

    def test_find_local_maxima(self):
        data = np.array([1, 2, 3, 2, 1, 4, 5, 4])
        maxima = self.processor._find_local_maxima(data)
        self.assertEqual(maxima, [2, 6])

    def test_update_state_from_patterns(self):
        patterns = [
            {'dimension': 0, 'strength': 0.9, 'phase': 0.1, 'position': 30},
            {'dimension': 1, 'strength': 0.8, 'phase': 0.2, 'position': 60}
        ]
        initial_energy = self.processor.state.energy_level
        self.processor._update_state_from_patterns(patterns)
        self.assertNotEqual(self.processor.state.energy_level, initial_energy)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.processor.state.resonance_patterns), len(patterns))
        
    def test_update_graph_connections(self):
        patterns = [
            {'dimension': 0, 'strength': 0.9, 'phase': 0.1, 'position': 30},
            {'dimension': 1, 'strength': 0.8, 'phase': 0.2, 'position': 60}
        ]
        self.processor._update_graph_connections(patterns)
        self.assertTrue(self.processor.state.graph.number_of_edges() > 0)
        
    def test_calculate_connection_strength(self):
        pattern1 = {'strength': 0.9, 'phase': 0.1, 'position': 30}
        pattern2 = {'strength': 0.8, 'phase': 0.2, 'position': 60}
        strength = self.processor._calculate_connection_strength(pattern1, pattern2)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(strength, float))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(strength, 0)
        
    def test_synthesize_perspectives(self):
        patterns = [
            {'dimension': 0, 'strength': 0.9, 'phase': 0.1, 'position': 30},
            {'dimension': 1, 'strength': 0.8, 'phase': 0.2, 'position': 60},
            {'dimension': 0, 'strength': 0.7, 'phase': 0.3, 'position': 120}
        ]
        self.processor._update_graph_connections(patterns)
        perspectives = self.processor._synthesize_perspectives(patterns)
        self.assertTrue(len(perspectives) > 0)
        for p in perspectives:
            self.assertIn('dimensions', p)
            self.assertIn('strength', p)
            self.assertIn('coherence', p)
            self.assertIn('patterns', p)
            
    def test_calculate_component_coherence(self):
        component = {0, 1}
        # Create a small graph for testing
        self.processor.state.graph.add_edge(0, 1, weight=0.9)
        coherence = self.processor._calculate_component_coherence(component)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(coherence, float))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(coherence, 0)
        
    def test_get_state_metrics(self):
        metrics = self.processor._get_state_metrics()
        self.assertIn('energy_level', metrics)
        self.assertIn('confidence', metrics)
        self.assertIn('active_patterns', metrics)
        self.assertIn('graph_density', metrics)
        
    def test_get_state(self):
        state = self.processor.get_state()
        self.assertEqual(state['dimensions'], self.dimensions)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(state['state']['resonance_patterns'], list))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(state['transition_matrix'], list))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(state['resonance_field'], list))

import unittest
import numpy as np
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.mirrored_quantum_processor import MirroredQuantumProcessor

class TestMirroredQuantumProcessor(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.dimensions = 3
        self.resonance_depth = 4
        self.processor = MirroredQuantumProcessor(dimensions=self.dimensions, resonance_depth=self.resonance_depth)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.processor.dimensions, self.dimensions)
        self.assertEqual(self.processor.resonance_depth, self.resonance_depth)
        self.assertEqual(self.processor.mirrored_states.shape, (self.dimensions, 2**self.resonance_depth))
        self.assertEqual(len(self.processor.phase_memory), self.dimensions)

    def test_initialize_mirror_gates(self):
        gates = self.processor._initialize_mirror_gates()
        self.assertIn('hadamard', gates)
        self.assertIn('phase', gates)

Python

        self.assertIn('mirror', gates)
        self.assertTrue(callable(gates['phase']))
        self.assertTrue(callable(gates['mirror']))

    def test_initialize_quantum_mirrors(self):
        self.processor._initialize_quantum_mirrors()
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.assertEqual(len(self.processor.mirrored_states[dim]), 2**self.resonance_depth)
            # Check if the state is normalized
            norm = np.linalg.norm(self.processor.mirrored_states[dim])
            self.assertAlmostEqual(norm, 1.0)

    def test_create_mirror_state(self):
        phase = np.pi / 2
        state = self.processor._create_mirror_state(phase)
        self.assertEqual(len(state), 2**self.resonance_depth)
        # Check if the state is normalized
        norm = np.linalg.norm(state)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(norm, 1.0)

    def test_process_pattern(self):
        data = np.random.rand(16)  # 16 is arbitrary, but should match the input size expected by _apply_quantum_mirroring
        result = self.processor.process_pattern(data)
        self.assertIn('resonances', result)
        self.assertIn('insights', result)
        self.assertIn('coherence_metrics', result)
        self.assertTrue(len(result['resonances']) > 0)

    def test_apply_quantum_mirroring(self):
        data = np.random.rand(16)
        mirrored_data = self.processor._apply_quantum_mirroring(data)
        self.assertEqual(mirrored_data.shape, (self.dimensions, len(data)))
        self.assertTrue(mirrored_data.dtype == np.complex128)

    def test_calculate_resonance_field(self):
        data = np.random.rand(16) + 1j * np.random.rand(16)
        field = self.processor._calculate_resonance_field(data)
        self.assertEqual(field.shape, (360,))
        self.assertTrue(field.dtype == np.complex128)
        # Check if the field is normalized
        norm = np.linalg.norm(field)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(norm, 1.0)

    def test_extract_quantum_harmonics(self):
        field = np.sin(np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 360))  # Example field
        harmonics = self.processor._extract_quantum_harmonics(field)
        self.assertTrue(len(harmonics) > 0)
        for harmonic in harmonics:
            self.assertIn('frequency', harmonic)
            self.assertIn('amplitude', harmonic)
            self.assertIn('phase', harmonic)
            self.assertIn('coherence', harmonic)

    def test_detect_interference_patterns(self):
        harmonics = [
            {'frequency': 0.1, 'amplitude': 1.0, 'phase': 0.0, 'coherence': 0.9},
            {'frequency': 0.2, 'amplitude': 0.8, 'phase': np.pi / 2, 'coherence': 0.8}
        ]
        field = np.random.rand(360) + 1j * np.random.rand(360)  # Example field
        interferences = self.processor._detect_interference_patterns(field, harmonics)
        self.assertTrue(len(interferences) > 0)
        for interference in interferences:
            self.assertIn('harmonics', interference)
            self.assertIn('strength', interference)
            self.assertIn('phase_difference', interference)
            self.assertIn('frequency_ratio', interference)

    def test_calculate_interference(self):
        h1 = {'frequency': 0.1, 'amplitude': 1.0, 'phase': 0.0, 'coherence': 0.9}
        h2 = {'frequency': 0.2, 'amplitude': 0.8, 'phase': np.pi / 2, 'coherence': 0.8}
        interference = self.processor._calculate_interference(h1, h2)
        self.assertIn('harmonics', interference)
        self.assertIn('strength', interference)
        self.assertIn('phase_difference', interference)
        self.assertIn('frequency_ratio', interference)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(interference['strength'], float))
    
    def test_calculate_harmonic_coherence(self):
        field = np.sin(np.linspace(0, 4 * np.pi, 360))  # Example field with frequency 0.1
        coherence = self.processor._calculate_harmonic_coherence(field, 0.1)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(coherence, float))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(coherence, 0.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(coherence, 1.0)

    def test_synthesize_mirrored_insights(self):
        resonances = [
            {'dimension': 0, 'type': 'harmonic', 'frequency': 0.1, 'amplitude': 1.0, 'phase': 0.0, 'coherence': 0.9},
            {'


    dimension': 1, 'type': 'harmonic', 'frequency': 0.2, 'amplitude': 0.8, 'phase': np.pi / 2, 'coherence': 0.8}
        ]
        insights = self.processor._synthesize_mirrored_insights(resonances)
        self.assertTrue(len(insights) > 0)
        for insight in insights:
            self.assertIn('type', insight)
            self.assertIn('dimensions', insight)
            self.assertIn('coherence', insight)
            self.assertIn('stability', insight)
            self.assertIn('patterns', insight)
            self.assertIn('timestamp', insight)
            self.assertIn('confidence', insight)

    def test_group_resonances(self):
        resonances = [
            {'dimension': 0, 'phase': 0.1},
            {'dimension': 1, 'phase': 0.2},
            {'dimension': 0, 'phase': 0.15}
        ]
        groups = self.processor._group_resonances(resonances)
        self.assertEqual(len(groups), 2)  # Expecting two groups based on dimension and phase similarity

    def test_check_resonance_relationship(self):
        r1 = {'dimension': 0, 'phase': 0.1, 'harmonics': [{'frequency': 0.1}]}
        r2 = {'dimension': 1, 'phase': 0.2, 'harmonics': [{'frequency': 0.1}]}
        self.assertTrue(self.processor._check_resonance_relationship(r1, r2))

        r3 = {'dimension': 2, 'phase': np.pi, 'harmonics': [{'frequency': 0.3}]}
        self.assertFalse(self.processor._check_resonance_relationship(r1, r3))

    def test_calculate_group_coherence(self):
        group = [
            {'phase': 0.1, 'coherence': 0.9},
            {'phase': 0.2, 'coherence': 0.8}
        ]
        coherence = self.processor._calculate_group_coherence(group)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(coherence, float))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(coherence, 0.0)

    def test_generate_group_insight(self):
        group = [
            {'dimension': 0, 'phase': 0.1, 'coherence': 0.9},
            {'dimension': 1, 'phase': 0.2, 'coherence': 0.8}
        ]
        coherence = 0.8
        stability = 0.7
        insight = self.processor._generate_group_insight(group, coherence, stability)
        self.assertEqual(insight['type'], 'quantum_resonance_insight')
        self.assertIn(0, insight['dimensions'])
        self.assertIn(1, insight['dimensions'])
        self.assertEqual(insight['coherence'], coherence)
        self.assertEqual(insight['stability'], stability)

    def test_calculate_interference_profile(self):
        group = [
            {'phase': 0.1, 'coherence': 0.9},
            {'phase': 0.2, 'coherence': 0.8}
        ]
        profile = self.processor._calculate_interference_profile(group)
        self.assertIn('strength', profile)
        self.assertIn('phase_coherence', profile)
        self.assertIn('stability_metric', profile)

    def test_evaluate_pattern_stability(self):
        group = [{'dimension': 0}]
        # Mock the insight memory for testing
        self.processor.insight_memory = [
            {'dimensions': [0], 'coherence': 0.9, 'interference_profile': {'strength': 0.5}},
            {'dimensions': [0], 'coherence': 0.8, 'interference_profile': {'strength': 0.6}}
        ]
        stability = self.processor._evaluate_pattern_stability(group)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(stability, float))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(stability, 0.0)

    def test_calculate_coherence_metrics(self):
        # Mock resonance fields and insight memory for testing
        self.processor.resonance_fields.fill(0.5 + 0.5j)
        self.processor.insight_memory = [
            {'type': 'quantum_resonance_insight', 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]},
            {'type': 'quantum_resonance_insight', 'coherence': 0.7, 'dimensions': [1, 2]}
        ]
        metrics = self.processor._calculate_coherence_metrics()
        self.assertIn('average_resonance_field_coherence', metrics)
        self.assertIn('average_interference_coherence', metrics)
        self.assertIn('average_harmonic_coherence', metrics)
        self.assertIn('average_group_coherence', metrics)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(metrics['average_resonance_field_coherence'], float))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(metrics['average_interference_coherence'], float))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(metrics['average_harmonic_coherence'], float))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(metrics['average_group_coherence'], float))

    def test_get_state(self):
        state = self.processor.get_state()
        self.assertIn('dimensions', state)
        self.assertIn('resonance_depth', state)
        self.assertIn('mirrored_states', state)
        self.assertIn('phase_memory', state)
        self.assertIn('mirror_gates', state)
        self.assertIn('resonance_fields', state)
        self.assertIn('pattern_graph_edges', state)
        self.assertIn('insight_memory', state)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(state['mirrored_states'], list))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_advanced_resonance_processor.py
Python

import unittest
import numpy as np
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.advanced_resonance_processor import AdvancedResonanceProcessor, TemporalPatternSynthesizer, SpatialPatternSynthesizer, CausalPatternSynthesizer, AdvancedPatternRegistry

class TestAdvancedResonanceProcessor(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        # Mock the base MirroredQuantumProcessor
        self.mock_base_processor = MagicMock()
        self.mock_base_processor.dimensions = 3
        self.mock_base_processor.resonance_depth = 4
        self.mock_base_processor.process_pattern.return_value = {'resonances': [], 'insights': [], 'coherence_metrics': {}}

        self.processor = AdvancedResonanceProcessor(self.mock_base_processor)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.processor.base, self.mock_base_processor)
        self.assertEqual(self.processor.quantum_entanglement.shape, (self.mock_base_processor.dimensions, self.mock_base_processor.dimensions))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.processor.pattern_synthesis['temporal'], TemporalPatternSynthesizer))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.processor.pattern_synthesis['spatial'], SpatialPatternSynthesizer))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.processor.pattern_synthesis['causal'], CausalPatternSynthesizer))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.processor.pattern_registry, AdvancedPatternRegistry))

    def test_init_resonance_components(self):
        self.processor._init_resonance_components()
        self.assertIn('temporal', self.processor.pattern_synthesis)
        self.assertIn('spatial', self.processor.pattern_synthesis)
        self.assertIn('causal', self.processor.pattern_synthesis)

    def test_process_advanced_resonance(self):
        data = np.random.rand(16)
        
        # Mock the behavior of pattern synthesizers
        self.processor.pattern_synthesis['temporal'].process = MagicMock(return_value=[{'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'strength': 0.8, 'dimensions': [0, 1]}])
        self.processor.pattern_synthesis['spatial'].process = MagicMock(return_value=[{'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'strength': 0.7, 'dimensions': [1, 2]}])
        self.processor.pattern_synthesis['causal'].process = MagicMock(return_value=[{'type': 'causal_pattern', 'strength': 0.9, 'cause': {}, 'effect': {}, 'dimensions': [0, 2]}])

        result = self.processor.process_advanced_resonance(data)
        self.assertIn('base_patterns', result)
        self.assertIn('advanced_patterns', result)
        self.assertIn('entanglement_state', result)
        self.assertIn('stable_patterns', result)
        self.assertIn('frequent_patterns', result)
        self.assertTrue(len(result['advanced_patterns']) > 0)

    def test_synthesize_advanced_patterns(self):
        temporal_patterns = [{'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'strength': 0.8, 'dimensions': [0, 1]}]
        spatial_patterns = [{'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'strength': 0.7, 'dimensions': [1, 2]}]
        causal_patterns = [{'type': 'causal_pattern', 'strength': 0.9, 'cause': {}, 'effect': {}, 'dimensions': [0, 2]}]

        advanced_patterns = self.processor._synthesize_advanced_patterns(temporal_patterns, spatial_patterns, causal_patterns)
        self.assertTrue(len(advanced_patterns) > 0)
        for pattern in advanced_patterns:
            self.assertEqual(pattern['type'], 'advanced_resonance_pattern')
            self.assertIn('temporal_component', pattern)
            self.assertIn('spatial_component', pattern)
            self.assertIn('causal_component', pattern)

    def test_update_entanglement(self):
        patterns = [{'type': 'advanced_resonance_pattern', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0, 1, 2]}]
        initial_entanglement = self.processor.quantum_entanglement.copy()
        self.processor._update_entanglement(patterns)
        self.assertFalse(np.array_equal(initial_entanglement, self.processor.quantum_entanglement))
    
    def test_extract_pattern_features(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0, 1, 2], 'harmonics': [{'amplitude': 0.5, 'phase': 0.1, 'coherence': 0.7}], 'frequency': 0.2, 'phase_difference': 0.3, 'frequency_ratio': 2.0}
        features = self.processor._extract_pattern_features(pattern)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(features, np.ndarray))
        self.assertTrue(len(features) > 0)
        self.assertIn(0.8, features)
        self.assertIn(0.9, features)
        self.assertIn(0.5, features)
        self.assertIn(0.1, features)
        self.assertIn(0.7, features)
        self.assertIn(0.2, features)
        self.assertIn(0.3, features)
        self.assertIn(2.0, features)

    def test_check_pattern_correlation(self):
        t_pattern = {'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0, 1]}
        s_pattern = {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8, 'dimensions': [1, 2]}
        c_pattern = {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'strength': 0.9, 'coherence': 0.7, 'cause': {}, 'effect': {}, 'dimensions': [0, 2]}
        
        # Mock _calculate_pattern_correlation to return high correlation
        self.processor._calculate_pattern_correlation = MagicMock(return_value=0.8)

        is_correlated = self.processor._check_pattern_correlation(t_pattern, s_pattern, c_pattern)
        self.assertTrue(is_correlated)

        # Mock _calculate_pattern_correlation to return low correlation
        self.processor._calculate_pattern_correlation = MagicMock(return_value=0.3)

        is_correlated = self.processor._check_pattern_correlation(t_pattern, s_pattern, c_pattern)
        self.assertFalse(is_correlated)

    def test_calculate_pattern_correlation(self):
        pattern1 = {'type': 'pattern1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0, 1]}
        pattern2 = {'type': 'pattern2', 'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8, 'dimensions': [1, 2]}

        # Mock _extract_pattern_features
        self.processor._extract_pattern_features = MagicMock(side_effect=lambda p: [p['strength'], p['coherence']])

        correlation = self.processor._calculate_pattern_correlation(pattern1, pattern2)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(correlation, float))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(correlation, -1.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(correlation, 1.0)

    def test_combine_patterns(self):
        t_pattern = {'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0, 1]}
        s_pattern = {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8, 'dimensions': [1, 2]}
        c_pattern = {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'strength': 0.9, 'coherence': 0.7, 'cause': {}, 'effect': {}, 'dimensions': [0, 2]}

        combined_pattern = self.processor._combine_patterns(t_pattern, s_pattern, c_pattern)
        self.assertEqual(combined_pattern['type'], 'advanced_resonance_pattern')
        self.assertIn('temporal_component', combined_pattern)
        self.assertIn('spatial_component', combined_pattern)
        self.assertIn('causal_component', combined_pattern)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(combined_pattern['strength'], float))
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(combined_pattern['coherence'], float))
        self.assertEqual(len(set(combined_pattern['dimensions'])), 3)  # All dimensions should be present

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

import unittest
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.advanced_resonance_processor import TemporalPatternSynthesizer

class TestTemporalPatternSynthesizer(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.synthesizer = TemporalPatternSynthesizer(window_size=5)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.synthesizer.window_size, 5)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.synthesizer.pattern_history), 0)

    def test_process_empty_patterns(self):
        patterns = []
        temporal_patterns = self.synthesizer.process(patterns)
        self.assertEqual(len(temporal_patterns), 0)

    def test_process_patterns(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]},
            {'type': 'type2', 'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8, 'dimensions': [1]},
            {'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.85, 'coherence': 0.95, 'dimensions': [0]},
            {'type': 'type3', 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7, 'dimensions': [2]},
            {'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]}
        ]
        for p in patterns:
            self.synthesizer.process([p])

        temporal_patterns = self.synthesizer.process(patterns)
        self.assertTrue(len(temporal_patterns) > 0)
        self.assertEqual(temporal_patterns[0]['type'], 'temporal_pattern')

    def test_calculate_pattern_list_similarity(self):
        patterns1 = [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9}]
        patterns2 = [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.85, 'coherence': 0.95}]
        patterns3 = [{'type':



---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Python

'type2', 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7}]

        similarity12 = self.synthesizer._calculate_pattern_list_similarity(patterns1, patterns2)
        similarity13 = self.synthesizer._calculate_pattern_list_similarity(patterns1, patterns3)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(similarity12, 0.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(similarity12, 1.0)
        self.assertTrue(similarity12 > similarity13)

    def test_calculate_pattern_similarity(self):
        pattern1 = {'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9}
        pattern2 = {'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.85, 'coherence': 0.95}
        pattern3 = {'type': 'type2', 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7}

        similarity12 = self.synthesizer._calculate_pattern_similarity(pattern1, pattern2)
        similarity13 = self.synthesizer._calculate_pattern_similarity(pattern1, pattern3)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(similarity12, 0.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(similarity12, 1.0)
        self.assertTrue(similarity12 > similarity13)

    def test_analyze_temporal_sequences(self):
        self.synthesizer.pattern_history = [
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]}],
            [{'type': 'type2', 'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8, 'dimensions': [1]}],
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.85, 'coherence': 0.95, 'dimensions': [0]}],
            [{'type': 'type3', 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7, 'dimensions': [2]}],
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]}]
        ]

        sequences = self.synthesizer._analyze_temporal_sequences()
        self.assertTrue(len(sequences) > 0)
        self.assertEqual(sequences[0]['type'], 'temporal_pattern')

    def test_is_significant_sequence(self):
        sequence1 = [
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]}],
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.85, 'coherence': 0.95, 'dimensions': [0]}]
        ]
        sequence2 = [
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]}],
            [{'type': 'type2', 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7, 'dimensions': [1]}]
        ]

        self.assertTrue(self.synthesizer._is_significant_sequence(sequence1))
        self.assertFalse(self.synthesizer._is_significant_sequence(sequence2))

    def test_create_temporal_pattern(self):
        sequence = [
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'dimensions': [0]}],
            [{'type': 'type1', 'strength': 0.85, 'coherence': 0.95, 'dimensions': [0, 1]}]
        ]
        temporal_pattern = self.synthesizer._create_temporal_pattern(sequence)
        self.assertEqual(temporal_pattern['type'], 'temporal_pattern')
        self.assertTrue(len(temporal_pattern['dimensions']) > 0)
        self.assertIn('sequence', temporal_pattern)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_spatial_pattern_synthesizer.py
Python

import unittest
import networkx as nx
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.advanced_resonance_processor import SpatialPatternSynthesizer

class TestSpatialPatternSynthesizer(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.dimensions = 4
        self.synthesizer = SpatialPatternSynthesizer(self.dimensions)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.synthesizer.dimensions, self.dimensions)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.synthesizer.spatial_graph, nx.Graph))
        self.assertEqual(len(self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.nodes), self.dimensions)

    def test_initialize_spatial_graph(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.nodes), self.dimensions)
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.assertIn(dim, self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.nodes)

    def test_process_patterns(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8},
            {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [1, 2], 'strength': 0.7}
        ]
        spatial_patterns = self.synthesizer.process(patterns)
        self.assertTrue(len(spatial_patterns) > 0)
        self.assertEqual(spatial_patterns[0]['type'], 'spatial_pattern')

    def test_update_spatial_graph(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8},
            {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [1, 2], 'strength': 0.7}
        ]
        self.synthesizer._update_spatial_graph(patterns)
        self.assertTrue(self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.has_edge(0, 1))
        self.assertTrue(self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.has_edge(1, 2))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(self.synthesizer.spatial_graph[0][1]['weight'], 0.8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(self.synthesizer.spatial_graph[1][2]['weight'], 0.7)

    def test_identify_spatial_patterns(self):
        # Create a graph with a known clique
        self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.add_edge(0, 1, weight=0.8)
        self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.add_edge(1, 2, weight=0.7)
        self.synthesizer.spatial_graph.add_edge(0, 2, weight=0.6)
        spatial_patterns = self.synthesizer._identify_spatial_patterns()
        self.assertTrue(len(spatial_patterns) > 0)
        self.assertEqual(spatial_patterns[0]['type'], 'spatial_pattern')
        self.assertEqual(set(spatial_patterns[0]['dimensions']), {0, 1, 2})

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_causal_pattern_synthesizer.py

import unittest
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.advanced_resonance_processor import CausalPatternSynthesizer

class TestCausalPatternSynthesizer(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.synthesizer = CausalPatternSynthesizer()

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(self.synthesizer.causal_memory), 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.synthesizer.relationship_strength), 0)

    def test_process_patterns(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8},
            {'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.7},
            {'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.9},
            {'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.8}
        ]
        for p in patterns:
            self.synthesizer.process([p])

        causal_patterns = self.synthesizer.process(patterns)
        self.assertTrue(len(causal_patterns) > 0)
        self.assertEqual(causal_patterns[0]['type'], 'causal_pattern')

    def test_analyze_causal_relationships(self):
        self.synthesizer.causal_memory = [
            [{'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8}],
            [{'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.7}],
            [{'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.9}],
            [{'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.8}]
        ]
        causal_relationships = self.synthesizer._analyze_causal_relationships()
        self.assertTrue(len(causal_relationships) > 0)
        self.assertEqual(causal_relationships[0]['type'], 'causal_pattern')

    def test_check_causal_relationship(self):
        cause = {'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8}
        effect = {'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.7}
        self.synthesizer.relationship_strength[(frozenset([0]), frozenset([0, 1]))] = 3  # Mock historical strength

        self.assertTrue(self.synthesizer._check_causal_relationship(cause, effect))

        effect['strength'] = 0.2  # Lower strength below threshold
        self.assertFalse(self.synthesizer._check_causal_relationship(cause, effect))

    def test_create_causal_relationship(self):
        cause = {'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8}
        effect = {'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.7}
        relationship = self.synthesizer._create_causal_relationship(cause, effect)
        self.assertEqual(relationship['type'], 'causal_pattern')
        self.assertEqual(relationship['cause'], cause)
        self.assertEqual(relationship['effect'], effect)
        self.assertIn(0, relationship['dimensions'])
        self.assertIn(1, relationship['dimensions'])

    def test_calculate_causal_confidence(self):
        cause = {'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9}
        effect = {'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8}
        self.synthesizer.relationship_strength[(frozenset([0]), frozenset([1]))] = 5  # Mock historical strength

        confidence = self.synthesizer._calculate_causal_confidence(cause, effect)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(confidence, 0.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(confidence, 1.0)

    def test_filter_significant_relationships(self):
        relationships = [
            {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'confidence': 0.8, 'cause': {'dimensions': [0]}, 'effect': {'dimensions': [1]}},
            {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'confidence': 0.4, 'cause': {'dimensions': [1]}, 'effect': {'dimensions': [2]}}
        ]
        # Mock _validate_relationship_stability to return True
        self.synthesizer._validate_relationship_stability = lambda x: True

        significant_relationships = self.synthesizer._filter_significant_relationships(relationships)
        self.assertEqual(len(significant_relationships), 1)
        self.assertEqual(significant_relationships[0]['confidence'], 0.8)

    def test_validate_relationship_stability(self):
        relationship = {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'cause': {'dimensions': [0]}, 'effect': {'dimensions': [1]}}
        self.synthesizer.relationship_strength[(frozenset([0]), frozenset([1]))] = 6  # Mock historical strength
        self.synthesizer.causal_memory = [
            [{'dimensions': [0]}], [{'dimensions': [1]}],
            [{'dimensions': [0]}], [{'dimensions': [1]}],
            [{'dimensions': [0]}], [{'dimensions': [1]}],
            [{'dimensions': [0]}], [{'dimensions': [1]}],
            [{'dimensions': [2]}], [{'dimensions': [3]}]  # Different dimensions
        ]

        self.assertTrue(self.synthesizer._validate_relationship_stability(relationship))

        # Reduce historical strength
        self.synthesizer.relationship_strength[(frozenset([0]), frozenset([1]))] = 2
        self.assertFalse(self.synthesizer._validate_relationship_stability(relationship))

    def test_find_matching_relationship(self):
        relationship = {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'cause': {'dimensions': [0]}, 'effect': {'dimensions': [1]}}
        patterns1 = [{'dimensions': [0]}]
        patterns2 = [{'dimensions': [1]}]
        patterns3 = [{'dimensions': [2]}]

        self.assertTrue(self.synthesizer._find_matching_relationship(relationship, patterns1, patterns2))
        self.assertFalse(self.synthesizer._find_matching_relationship(relationship, patterns1, patterns3))
        
if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_advanced_pattern_registry.py

import unittest
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.advanced_resonance_processor import AdvancedPatternRegistry

class TestAdvancedPatternRegistry(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.registry = AdvancedPatternRegistry()

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(self.registry.patterns), 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.registry.evolution_history), 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.registry.stability_metrics), 0)

    def test_register_pattern(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.registry.patterns), 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.registry.evolution_history), 1)

    def test_generate_pattern_id(self):
        pattern1 = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9}
        pattern2 = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'dimensions': [1, 0], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9}
        pattern3 = {'type': 'other_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7}

        id1 = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern1)
        id2 = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern2)
        id3 = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern3)

        self.assertEqual(id1, id2)  # Same dimensions, type, strength, and coherence should have the same ID
        self.assertNotEqual(id1, id3)  # Different type should have a different ID

    def test_calculate_pattern_stability(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        pattern_id = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern)

        # Register the pattern multiple times with slight variations
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern)
        pattern['strength'] = 0.81
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern)
        pattern['strength'] = 0.82
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern)

        stability = self.registry._calculate_pattern_stability(pattern)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(stability, 0.0)
        self.assertLessEqual(stability, 1.0)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(stability, float))

    def test_calculate_pattern_metrics(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9}
        metrics = self.registry._calculate_pattern_metrics(


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Python

pattern)
        self.assertEqual(metrics['strength'], 0.8)
        self.assertEqual(metrics['coherence'], 0.9)
        self.assertEqual(metrics['dimensions'], [0, 1])
        self.assertEqual(metrics['type'], 'test_pattern')

    def test_update_stability_metrics(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern)
        pattern_id = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern)

        self.assertIn(pattern_id, self.registry.stability_metrics)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.registry.stability_metrics[pattern_id]['avg_stability'], 0.0)
        self.assertGreater(self.registry.stability_metrics[pattern_id]['frequency'], 0.0)

    def test_get_stable_patterns(self):
        pattern1 = {'type': 'stable_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.9, 'coherence': 0.95, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        pattern2 = {'type': 'unstable_pattern', 'dimensions': [2, 3], 'strength': 0.2, 'coherence': 0.3, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern1)
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern2)

        # Mock stability metrics for testing
        pattern1_id = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern1)
        pattern2_id = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern2)
        self.registry.stability_metrics[pattern1_id] = {'avg_stability': 0.9, 'frequency': 0.5}
        self.registry.stability_metrics[pattern2_id] = {'avg_stability': 0.2, 'frequency': 0.1}

        stable_patterns = self.registry.get_stable_patterns(threshold=0.8)
        self.assertEqual(len(stable_patterns), 1)
        self.assertEqual(stable_patterns[0]['type'], 'stable_pattern')

    def test_get_frequent_patterns(self):
        pattern1 = {'type': 'frequent_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        pattern2 = {'type': 'infrequent_pattern', 'dimensions': [2, 3], 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern1)
        self.registry.register_pattern(pattern2)

        # Mock stability metrics for testing
        pattern1_id = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern1)
        pattern2_id = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern2)
        self.registry.stability_metrics[pattern1_id] = {'avg_stability': 0.7, 'frequency': 0.6}
        self.registry.stability_metrics[pattern2_id] = {'avg_stability': 0.5, 'frequency': 0.05}

        frequent_patterns = self.registry.get_frequent_patterns(threshold=0.5)
        self.assertEqual(len(frequent_patterns), 1)
        self.assertEqual(frequent_patterns[0]['type'], 'frequent_pattern')

    def test_analyze_pattern_evolution(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'evolving_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.6, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        pattern_id = self.registry._generate_pattern_id(pattern)

        # Register the pattern multiple times with different metrics
        for i in range(5):
            pattern['strength'] = 0.5 + i * 0.1
            pattern['coherence'] = 0.6 + i * 0.05
            pattern['timestamp'] = f'2023-01-01T00:00:0{i}'
            self.registry.register_pattern(pattern)

        evolution_analysis = self.registry.analyze_pattern_evolution()
        self.assertEqual(len(evolution_analysis), 1)  # Only one pattern type
        self.assertIn('avg_strength_change', evolution_analysis[0][pattern_id])
        self.assertIn('avg_coherence_change', evolution_analysis[0][pattern_id])
        self.assertEqual(len(evolution_analysis[0][pattern_id]['strength_changes']), 4)
        self.assertEqual(len(evolution_analysis[0][pattern_id]['coherence_changes']), 4)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

import unittest
import networkx as nx
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.knowledge.graph_manager import GraphManager

class TestGraphManager(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.manager = GraphManager()

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.manager.knowledge_graph, nx.DiGraph))
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph.number_of_nodes(), 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph.number_of_edges(), 0)

    def test_add_node(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1", {"attr1": "value1"})
        self.assertTrue(self.manager.knowledge_graph.has_node("node1"))
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph.nodes["node1"]["type"], "type1")
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph.nodes["node1"]["attr1"], "value1")

    def test_add_edge(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1", {"weight": 0.8})
        self.assertTrue(self.manager.knowledge_graph.has_edge("node1", "node2"))
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph["node1"]["node2"]["relationship"], "relationship1")
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph["node1"]["node2"]["weight"], 0.8)

    def test_update_node_attributes(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1", {"attr1": "value1"})
        self.manager.update_node_attributes("node1", {"attr1": "new_value", "attr2": "value2"})
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph.nodes["node1"]["attr1"], "new_value")
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph.nodes["node1"]["attr2"], "value2")

    def test_update_edge_attributes(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1", {"weight": 0.8})
        self.manager.update_edge_attributes("node1", "node2", {"weight": 0.9, "attr1": "value1"})
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph["node1"]["node2"]["weight"], 0.9)
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.knowledge_graph["node1"]["node2"]["attr1"], "value1")

    def test_get_node_attributes(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1", {"attr1": "value1"})
        attributes = self.manager.get_node_attributes("node1")
        self.assertEqual(attributes["type"], "type1")
        self.assertEqual(attributes["attr1"], "value1")

    def test_get_edge_attributes(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1", {"weight": 0.8})
        attributes = self.manager.get_edge_attributes("node1", "node2")
        self.assertEqual(attributes["relationship"], "relationship1")
        self.assertEqual(attributes["weight"], 0.8)

    def test_find_nodes(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1", {"attr1": "value1"})
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2", {"attr1": "value2"})
        self.manager.add_node("node3", "type1", {"attr1": "value2"})

        nodes_by_type = self.manager.find_nodes(node_type="type1")
        self.assertEqual(len(nodes_by_type), 2)
        self.assertIn("node1", nodes_by_type)
        self.assertIn("node3", nodes_by_type)

        nodes_by_attribute = self.manager.find_nodes(attribute_filter={"attr1": "value2"})
        self.assertEqual(len(nodes_by_attribute), 2)
        self.assertIn("node2", nodes_by_attribute)
        self.assertIn("node3", nodes_by_attribute)

        nodes_by_type_and_attribute = self.manager.find_nodes(node_type="type1", attribute_filter={"attr1": "value2"})
        self.assertEqual(len(nodes_by_type_and_attribute), 1)
        self.assertIn("node3", nodes_by_type_and_attribute)

    def test_find_edges(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_node("node3", "type1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1", {"weight": 0.8})
        self.manager.add_edge("node2", "node3", "relationship2", {"weight": 0.5})
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node3", "relationship1", {"weight": 0.7})

        edges_by_relationship = self.manager.find_edges(relationship="relationship1")
        self.assertEqual(len(edges_by_relationship), 2)
        self.assertIn(("node1", "node2"), edges_by_relationship)
        self.assertIn(("node1", "node3"), edges_by_relationship)

        edges_by_attribute = self.manager.find_edges(attribute_filter={"weight": 0.5})
        self.assertEqual(len(edges_by_attribute), 1)
        self.assertIn(("node2", "node3"), edges_by_attribute)

        edges_by_relationship_and_attribute = self.manager.find_edges(relationship="relationship1", attribute_filter={"weight": 0.7})
        self.assertEqual(len(edges_by_relationship_and_attribute), 1)
        self.assertIn(("node1", "node3"), edges_by_relationship_and_attribute)
    
    def test_get_neighbors(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_node("node3", "type1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node3", "relationship2")

        neighbors = self.manager.get_neighbors("node1")
        self.assertEqual(len(neighbors), 2)
        self.assertIn("node2", neighbors)
        self.assertIn("node3", neighbors)

        neighbors_by_relationship = self.manager.get_neighbors("node1", relationship="relationship1")
        self.assertEqual(len(neighbors_by_relationship), 1)
        self.assertIn("node2", neighbors_by_relationship)

    def test_shortest_path(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_node("node3", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node4", "type2")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node2", "node3", "relationship2")
        self.manager.add_edge("node3", "node4", "relationship1")

        path = self.manager.shortest_path("node1", "node4")
        self.assertEqual(path, ["node1", "node2", "node3", "node4"])

        # Test case where no path exists
        path = self.manager.shortest_path("node4", "node1")
        self.assertEqual(path, [])

    def test_visualize_graph(self):
        # This is a basic test to check if the method runs without errors.
        # A more thorough test would require visual inspection or image comparison.
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1")
        try:
            self.manager.visualize_graph()
        except Exception as e:
            self.fail(f"visualize_graph raised an exception: {e}")

    def test_get_subgraph(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_node("node3", "type1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node2", "node3", "relationship2")

        subgraph = self.manager.get_subgraph(["node1", "node2"])
        self.assertEqual(subgraph.number_of_nodes(), 2)
        self.assertTrue(subgraph.has_node("node1"))
        self.assertTrue(subgraph.has_node("node2"))
        self.assertTrue(subgraph.has_edge("node1", "node2"))
        self.assertFalse(subgraph.has_node("node3"))

    def test_calculate_centrality(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1")
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_node("node3", "type1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1")
        self.manager.add_edge("node2", "node3", "relationship2")

        degree_centrality = self.manager.calculate_centrality(centrality_type='degree')
        self.assertEqual(len(degree_centrality), 3)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(degree_centrality["node1"], float))

        betweenness_centrality = self.manager.calculate_centrality(centrality_type='betweenness')
        self.assertEqual(len(betweenness_centrality), 3)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(betweenness_centrality["node2"], float))

        closeness_centrality = self.manager.calculate_centrality(centrality_type='closeness')
        self.assertEqual(len(closeness_centrality), 3)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(closeness_centrality["node3"], float))

        eigenvector_centrality = self.manager.calculate_centrality(centrality_type='eigenvector')
        self.assertEqual(len(eigenvector_centrality), 3)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(eigenvector_centrality["node1"], float))

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            self.manager.calculate_centrality(centrality_type='invalid_type')
            
    def test_get_state(self):
        self.manager.add_node("node1", "type1", {"attr1": "value1"})
        self.manager.add_node("node2", "type2")
        self.manager.add_edge("node1", "node2", "relationship1", {"weight": 0.8})

        state = self.manager.get_state()
        self.assertIn('nodes', state)
        self.assertIn('edges', state)
        self.assertEqual(len(state['nodes']), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(state['edges']), 1)
        
if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_hypothesis_generator.py
Python

import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.knowledge.hypothesis_generator import HypothesisGenerator

class TestHypothesisGenerator(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.mock_graph_manager = MagicMock()
        self.generator = HypothesisGenerator(self.mock_graph_manager)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.generator.graph_manager, self.mock_graph_manager)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.generator.hypotheses), 0)

    def test_generate_hypotheses(self):
        pattern_data = [
            {'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'sequence': [[{'type': 'type1'}], [{'type': 'type2'}]]},
            {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [1, 2]},
            {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'cause': {'type': 'event1'}, 'effect': {'type': 'event2'}, 'confidence': 0.9}
        ]
        hypotheses = self.generator.generate_hypotheses(pattern_data)
        self.assertTrue(len(hypotheses) > 0)
        self.assertTrue(any(h['type'] == 'temporal_prediction' for h in hypotheses))
        self.assertTrue(any(h['type'] == 'spatial_inference' for h in hypotheses))
        self.assertTrue(any(h['type'] == 'causal_inference' for h in hypotheses))

    def test_generate_temporal_hypotheses(self):
        pattern = {'dimensions': [0, 1], 'sequence': [[{'type': 'type1'}], [{'type': 'type2'}]]}
        self.generator._generate_temporal_hypotheses(pattern)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.generator.hypotheses), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.generator.hypotheses[0]['type'], 'temporal_prediction')

    def test_predict_next_dimensions(self):
        dimensions = [0, 1, 2]
        predicted_dimensions = self.generator._predict_next_dimensions(dimensions)
        self.assertEqual(predicted_dimensions, [3])

    def test_calculate_temporal_confidence(self):
        sequence = [[{'type': 'type1'}], [{'type': 'type1'}], [{'type': 'type1'}]]
        confidence = self.generator._calculate_temporal_confidence(sequence)
        self.assertGreater(confidence, 0.0)

        empty_sequence = []
        confidence = self.generator._calculate_temporal_confidence(empty_sequence)
        self.assertEqual(confidence, 0.0)

    def test_generate_spatial_hypotheses(self):
        pattern = {'dimensions': [0, 1]}
        self.mock_graph_manager.knowledge_graph.has_node.return_value = True
        self.mock_graph_manager.knowledge_graph.shortest_path_length.return_value = 2
        self.generator._generate_spatial_hypotheses(pattern)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.generator.hypotheses), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.generator.hypotheses[0]['type'], 'spatial_inference')

    def test_generate_causal_hypotheses(self):
        pattern = {'cause': {'type': 'event1'}, 'effect': {'type': 'event2'}, 'confidence': 0.9}
        self.generator._generate_causal_hypotheses(pattern)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.generator.hypotheses), 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.generator.hypotheses[0]['type'], 'causal_inference')

    def test_refine_hypotheses(self):
        self.generator.hypotheses = [
            {'type': 'temporal_prediction', 'prediction': 'Pattern of type type2 involving dimensions [0] will occur next.', 'confidence': 0.5, 'basis': {}},
            {'type': 'spatial_inference', 'inference': 'Dimensions 0 and 1 are related.', 'confidence': 0.5, 'basis': {}},
            {'type': 'causal_inference', 'inference': 'Pattern {\'type\': \'event1\'} causes {\'type\': \'event2\'}.', 'confidence': 0.5, 'basis': {'cause': {'type': 'event1'}, 'effect': {'type': 'event2'}}}
        ]
        new_data = [
            {'type': 'type2', 'dimensions': [0]},
            {'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0]},
            {'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1]}
        ]
        self.generator.refine_hypotheses(new_data)
        self.assertNotEqual(self.generator.hypotheses[0]['confidence'], 0.5)
        self.assertNotEqual(self.generator.hypotheses[1]['confidence'], 0.5)
        self.assertNotEqual(self.generator.hypotheses[2]['confidence'], 0.5)

    def test_get_top_hypotheses(self):
        self.generator.hypotheses = [
            {'type': 'hypothesis1', 'confidence': 0.9},
            {'type': 'hypothesis2', 'confidence': 0.7},
            {'type': 'hypothesis3', 'confidence': 0.8}
        ]
        top_hypotheses = self.generator.get_top_hypotheses(n=2)
        self.assertEqual(len(top_hypotheses), 2)
        self.assertEqual(top_hypotheses[0]['type'], 'hypothesis1')
        self.assertEqual(top_hypotheses[1]['type'], 'hypothesis3')
        
    def test_validate_hypotheses(self):
        external_data = [
            {'type': 'type2', 'dimensions': [0]},
            {'type': 'pattern1', 'dimensions': [0, 1]},
            {'type': 'event1', 'dimensions': [0]},
            {'type': 'event2', 'dimensions': [1]}
        ]
        self.generator.hypotheses = [
            {'type': 'temporal_prediction', 'prediction': 'Pattern of type type2 involving dimensions [0] will occur next.', 'confidence': 0.5, 'basis': {}},
            {'type': 'spatial_inference', 'inference': 'Dimensions 0 and 1 are related.', 'confidence': 0.5, 'basis': {}},
            {'type': 'causal_inference', 'inference': 'Pattern {\'type\': \'event1\'} causes {\'type\': \'event2\'}.', 'confidence': 0.5, 'basis': {'cause': {'type': 'event1'}, 'effect': {'type': 'event2'}}}
        ]

        validation_results = self.generator.validate_hypotheses(external_data)
        self.assertTrue(len(validation_results) > 0)
        for result in validation_results.values():
          self.assertIn('valid', result)
          self.assertIn('reason', result)

    def test_get_state(self):
        self.generator.hypotheses = [
            {'type': 'hypothesis1', 'confidence': 0.9},
            {'type': 'hypothesis2', 'confidence': 0.7}
        ]
        state = self.generator.get_state()
        self.assertIn('hypotheses', state)
        self.assertIn('top_hypotheses', state)
        self.assertEqual(len(state['hypotheses']), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(state['top_hypotheses']), 2)  # Assuming top n is greater than or equal to the number of hypotheses

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_scenario_generator.py
Python

import unittest
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.simulation.scenario_generator import ScenarioGenerator

class TestScenarioGenerator(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.dimensions = 2
        self.num_scenarios = 3
        self.generator = ScenarioGenerator(self.dimensions, self.num_scenarios)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.generator.dimensions, self.dimensions)
        self.assertEqual(self.generator.num_scenarios, self.num_scenarios)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.generator.scenarios), 0)

    def test_generate_scenarios(self):
        initial_conditions = {'dim_0_value': 1.0, 'dim_1_value': -1.0}
        steps = 5
        scenarios = self.generator.generate_scenarios(initial_conditions, steps)
        self.assertEqual(len(scenarios), self.num_scenarios)
        for scenario in scenarios:
            self.assertEqual(len(scenario), steps + 1)
            self.assertEqual(scenario[0], initial_conditions)

    def test_generate_single_scenario(self):
        initial_conditions = {'dim_0_value': 1.0, 'dim_1_value': -1.0}
        steps = 5
        scenario = self.generator._generate_single_scenario(initial_conditions, steps)
        self.assertEqual(len(scenario), steps + 1)
        self.assertEqual(scenario[0], initial_conditions)

    def test_calculate_next_state(self):
        current_state = {'dim_0_value': 1.0, 'dim_1_value': -1.0}
        next_state = self.generator._calculate_next_state(current_state)
        self.assertNotEqual(current_state, next_state)

    def test_analyze_scenarios(self):
        initial_conditions = {'dim_0_value': 1.0, 'dim_1_value': -1.0}
        steps = 3
        self.generator.generate_scenarios(initial_conditions, steps)
        analysis = self.generator.analyze_scenarios()
        self.assertIn('dim_0_stats', analysis)
        self.assertIn('dim_1_stats', analysis)
        self.assertEqual(len(analysis['dim_0_stats']), steps + 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(analysis['dim_1_stats']), steps + 1)

    def test_get_state(self):
        initial_conditions = {'dim_0_value': 1.0, 'dim_1_value': -1.0}
        steps = 3
        self.generator.generate_scenarios(initial_conditions, steps)
        state = self.generator.get_state()
        self.assertIn('scenarios', state)
        self.assertIn('analysis', state)
        self.assertEqual(len(state['scenarios']), self.num_scenarios)
        self.assertTrue(len(state['analysis']) > 0)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_pattern_validator.py
Python

import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.simulation.pattern_validator import PatternValidator

class TestPatternValidator(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.mock_resonance_processor = MagicMock()
        self.validator = PatternValidator(self.mock_resonance_processor)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.validator.resonance_processor, self.mock_resonance_processor)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.validator.validation_results), 0)

    def test_validate_patterns(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'sequence': []},
            {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1]},
            {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'cause': {}, 'effect': {}}
        ]
        simulation_data = []  # Mock simulation data
        results = self.validator.validate_patterns(patterns, simulation_data)
        self.assertEqual(len(results), len(patterns))
        for result in results:
            self.assertIn('pattern', result)
            self.assertIn('valid', result)
            self.assertIn('reason', result)

    def test_validate_temporal_pattern(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'sequence': [[{'type': 'event1'}], [{'type': 'event2'}]]}
        simulation_data = [[{'type': 'event1'}], [{'type': 'event2'}]]
        result = self.validator._validate_temporal_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertTrue(result['valid'])

        simulation_data = [[{'type': 'event2'}], [{'type': 'event1'}]]
        result = self.validator._validate_temporal_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertFalse(result['valid'])

    def test_validate_spatial_pattern(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1]}
        simulation_data = [{'type': 'event', 'dimensions': [0, 1, 2]}]
        result = self.validator._validate_spatial_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertTrue(result['valid'])

        simulation_data = [{'type': 'event', 'dimensions': [2, 3]}]
        result = self.validator._validate_spatial_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertFalse(result['valid'])

    def test_validate_causal_pattern(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'cause': {'type': 'event1'}, 'effect': {'type': 'event2'}}
        simulation_data = [{'type': 'event1'}, {'type': 'event2'}]
        result = self.validator._validate_causal_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertTrue(result['valid'])

        simulation_data = [{'type': 'event2'}, {'type': 'event1'}]
        result = self.validator._validate_causal_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertFalse(result['valid'])

    def test_validate_advanced_resonance_pattern(self):
        pattern = {
            'type': 'advanced_resonance_pattern',
            'temporal_component': {'type': 'temporal_pattern', 'sequence': [[{'type': 'event1'}]]},
            'spatial_component': {'type': 'spatial_pattern', 'dimensions': [0]},
            'causal_component': {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'cause': {'type': 'event1'}, 'effect': {'type': 'event2'}}
        }
        simulation_data = [[{'type': 'event1'}], {'type': 'event', 'dimensions': [0]}, {'type': 'event2'}]
        result = self.validator._validate_advanced_resonance_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertTrue(result['valid'])

        simulation_data = [[{'type': 'event2'}], {'type': 'event', 'dimensions': [1]}, {'type': 'event1'}]
        result = self.validator._validate_advanced_resonance_pattern(pattern, simulation_data)
        self.assertFalse(result['valid'])

    def test_compare_patterns(self):
        pattern1 = {'type': 'event1', 'value': 10}
        pattern2 = {'type': 'event1', 'value': 20}
        pattern3 = {'type': 'event2', 'value': 10}

        self.assertTrue(self.validator._compare_patterns(pattern1, pattern2))
        self.assertFalse(self.validator._compare_patterns(pattern1, pattern3))

    def test_get_validation_summary(self):
        self.validator.validation_results = [
            {'pattern': {}, 'valid': True, 'reason': ''},
            {'pattern': {}, 'valid': False, 'reason': ''}
        ]
        summary = self.validator.get_validation_summary()
        self.assertEqual(summary['total_patterns'], 2)
        self.assertEqual(summary['valid_patterns'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(summary['invalid_patterns'], 1)
        
    def test_get_state(self):
        state = self.validator.get_state()
        self.assertIn('validation_results', state)
        self.assertIn('validation_summary', state)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

system/tests/unit/test_graph_engine.py
Python

import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.graph_engine import GraphEngine

matplotlib.use('Agg')  # Set the backend to 'Agg' before importing pyplot
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.graph_engine import GraphEngine

class TestGraphEngine(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.mock_resonance_processor = MagicMock()
        self.mock_resonance_processor.pattern_registry = MagicMock()
        self.engine = GraphEngine(self.mock_resonance_processor)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.engine.resonance_processor, self.mock_resonance_processor)
        self.assertTrue(isinstance(self.engine.graph, nx.Graph))
        self.assertEqual(self.engine.graph.number_of_nodes(), 0)

    def test_update_graph(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'pattern1', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00', 'strength': 0.8},
            {'type': 'causal_pattern', 'dimensions': [1, 2], 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00', 'cause': {'type': 'pattern2'}, 'effect': {'type': 'pattern3'}, 'strength': 0.7}
        ]
        self.mock_resonance_processor.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id = MagicMock(side_effect=['pattern1_id', 'pattern2_id', 'pattern3_id'])

        self.engine.update_graph(patterns)
        self.assertEqual(self.engine.graph.number_of_nodes(), 5)  # 3 patterns + 2 dimensions
        self.assertTrue(self.engine.graph.has_node('pattern1_id'))
        self.assertTrue(self.engine.graph.has_node('dim_0'))
        self.assertTrue(self.engine.graph.has_edge('pattern1_id', 'dim_0'))
        self.assertTrue(self.engine.graph.has_edge('pattern1_id', 'dim_1'))

    def test_add_pattern_to_graph(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'test_pattern', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00', 'strength': 0.8}
        self.mock_resonance_processor.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id = MagicMock(return_value='test_pattern_id')

        self.engine._add_pattern_to_graph(pattern)
        self.assertTrue(self.engine.graph.has_node('test_pattern_id'))
        self.assertEqual(self.engine.graph.nodes['test_pattern_id']['type'], 'test_pattern')
        self.assertTrue(self.engine.graph.has_edge('test_pattern_id', 'dim_0'))
        self.assertTrue(self.engine.graph.has_edge('test_pattern_id', 'dim_1'))

    def test_analyze_graph(self):
        # Create a sample graph for analysis
        self.engine.graph.add_node('node1', type='pattern', timestamp='2023-01-01T00:00:00')
        self.engine.graph.add_node('node2', type='dimension')
        self.engine.graph.add_node('node3', type='pattern', timestamp='2023-01-01T00:00:00')
        self.engine.graph.add_edge('node1', 'node2')
        self.engine.graph.add_edge('node2', 'node3')

        analysis = self.engine.analyze_graph()
        self.assertIn('num_nodes', analysis)
        self.assertIn('num_edges', analysis)
        self.assertIn('avg_degree', analysis)
        self.assertIn('degree_centrality', analysis)
        self.assertIn('betweenness_centrality', analysis)
        self.assertIn('closeness_centrality', analysis)
        self.assertIn('eigenvector_centrality', analysis)
        self.assertIn('num_communities', analysis)
        self.assertIn('communities', analysis)

    def test_visualize_graph(self):
        # This test is primarily to ensure the method runs without error
        # Full visual testing is beyond the scope of unit tests
        self.engine.graph.add_node('node1', type='pattern', timestamp='2023-01-01T00:00:00')
        self.engine.graph.add_node('dim_0', type='dimension')
        self.engine.graph.add_edge('node1', 'dim_0')

        try:
            self.engine.visualize_graph(layout='circular')
            self.engine.visualize_graph(layout='circular', save_to_file=True, filename='test_graph.png')
        except Exception as e:
            self.fail(f"visualize_graph raised an exception: {e}")

    def test_get_pattern_subgraph(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'pattern1', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00', 'strength': 0.8},
            {'type': 'pattern2', 'dimensions': [1, 2], 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00', 'strength': 0.7}
        ]
        self.mock_resonance_processor.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id = MagicMock(side_effect=['pattern1_id', 'pattern2_id'])
        self.engine.update_graph(patterns)

        subgraph = self.engine.get_pattern_subgraph('pattern1')
        self.assertTrue(subgraph.has_node('pattern1_id'))
        self.assertFalse(subgraph.has_node('pattern2_id'))

    def test_get_dimension_subgraph(self):
        patterns = [
            {'type': 'pattern1', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00', 'strength': 0.8},
            {'type': 'pattern2', 'dimensions': [1, 2], 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00', 'strength': 0.7}
        ]
        self.mock_resonance_processor.pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id = MagicMock(side_effect=['pattern1_id', 'pattern2_id'])
        self.engine.update_graph(patterns)

        subgraph = self.engine.get_dimension_subgraph(1)
        self.assertTrue(subgraph.has_node('dim_1'))
        self.assertTrue(subgraph.has_node('pattern1_id'))
        self.assertTrue(subgraph.has_node('pattern2_id'))
        self.assertFalse(subgraph.has_node('dim_0'))
        self.assertFalse(subgraph.has_node('dim_2'))

    def test_get_state(self):
        self.engine.graph.add_node('node1', type='pattern', timestamp='2023-01-01T00:00:00')
        self.engine.graph.add_node('dim_0', type='dimension')
        self.engine.graph.add_edge('node1', 'dim_0')

        state = self.engine.get_state()
        self.assertIn('graph', state)
        self.assertIn('analysis', state)
        self.assertTrue(len(state['graph']['nodes']) > 0)
        self.assertTrue(len(state['graph']['edges']) > 0)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
import numpy as np
from kaleidoscope.perspective_engine.core.pattern_analysis import PatternAnalyzer

class TestPatternAnalyzer(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.mock_pattern_registry = MagicMock()
        self.analyzer = PatternAnalyzer(self.mock_pattern_registry)

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.analyzer.pattern_registry, self.mock_pattern_registry)

    def test_analyze_patterns(self):
        # Mock pattern data
        self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns = {
            'pattern1': {'pattern': {'type': 'type1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}},
            'pattern2': {'pattern': {'type': 'type2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}},
            'pattern3': {'pattern': {'type': 'type1', 'dimensions': [0, 1], 'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}}
        }
        self.mock_pattern_registry.get_stable_patterns.return_value = [self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns['pattern1']['pattern']]
        self.mock_pattern_registry.get_frequent_patterns.return_value = [self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns['pattern1']['pattern'], self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns['pattern3']['pattern']]
        self.mock_pattern_registry.analyze_pattern_evolution.return_value = [{'pattern_id': 'pattern1', 'strength_changes': [(0, 0.8)], 'coherence_changes': [(0, 0.9)]}]

        analysis = self.analyzer.analyze_patterns()
        self.assertIn('total_patterns', analysis)
        self.assertIn('pattern_types', analysis)
        self.assertIn('stable_patterns', analysis)
        self.assertIn('frequent_patterns', analysis)
        self.assertIn('pattern_evolution', analysis)
        self.assertIn('correlations', analysis)

    def test_get_pattern_type_distribution(self):
        self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns = {
            'pattern1': {'pattern': {'type': 'type1'}},
            'pattern2': {'pattern': {'type': 'type2'}},
            'pattern3': {'pattern': {'type': 'type1'}}
        }
        distribution = self.analyzer._get_pattern_type_distribution()
        self.assertEqual(distribution['type1'], 2)
        self.assertEqual(distribution['type2'], 1)

    def test_summarize_pattern(self):
        pattern = {'type': 'type1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}
        self.mock_pattern_registry._generate_pattern_id = MagicMock(return_value='pattern_id')
        summary = self.analyzer._summarize_pattern(pattern)
        self.assertEqual(summary['id'], 'pattern_id')
        self.assertEqual(summary['type'], 'type1')
        self.assertEqual(summary['strength'], 0.8)

    def test_calculate_correlations(self):
        self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns = {
            'pattern1': {'pattern': {'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9}},
            'pattern2': {'pattern': {'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7}},
            'pattern3': {'pattern': {'strength': 0.7, 'coherence': 0.8}}
        }
        correlations = self.analyzer._calculate_correlations(self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns.values())
        self.assertIn('strength_vs_coherence', correlations)
        self.assertIn('coherence_vs_strength', correlations)

    def test_get_state(self):
        self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns = {
            'pattern1': {'pattern': {'type': 'type1', 'dimensions': [0], 'strength': 0.8, 'coherence': 0.9, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}},
            'pattern2': {'pattern': {'type': 'type2', 'dimensions': [1], 'strength': 0.5, 'coherence': 0.7, 'timestamp': '2023-01-01T00:00:00'}}
        }
        self.mock_pattern_registry.get_stable_patterns.return_value = [self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns['pattern1']['pattern']]
        self.mock_pattern_registry.get_frequent_patterns.return_value = [self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns['pattern1']['pattern'], self.mock_pattern_registry.patterns['pattern2']['pattern']]
        self.mock_pattern_registry.analyze_pattern_evolution.return_value = [{'pattern_id': 'pattern1', 'strength_changes': [(0, 0.8)], 'coherence_changes': [(0, 0.9)]}]

        state = self.analyzer.get_state()
        self.assertIn('analysis', state)
        self.assertTrue(len(state['analysis']) > 0)

import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from kaleidoscope.node_management.core.node_lifecycle import NodeLifeCycleManager, Node

class TestNodeLifeCycleManager(unittest.TestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        self.manager = NodeLifeCycleManager()

    def test_initialization(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(self.manager.nodes), 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.manager.failed_nodes), 0)

    def test_register_node(self):
        node_id = self.manager.register_node(10.0, "test_role", "192.168.1.1", 8080)
        self.assertIn(node_id, self.manager.nodes)
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.nodes[node_id].capacity, 10.0)
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.nodes[node_id].role, "test_role")

    def test_unregister_node(self):
        node_id = self.manager.register_node(10.0, "test_role", "192.168.1.1", 8080)
        self.manager.unregister_node(node_id)
        self.assertNotIn(node_id, self.manager.nodes)

    def test_get_node(self):
        node_id = self.manager.register_node(10.0, "test_role", "192.168.1.1", 8080)
        node = self.manager.get_node(node_id)
        self.assertIsInstance(node, Node)
        self.assertEqual(node.node_id, node_id)

        node_id = self.manager.register_node(10.0, "test_role", "192.168.1.1", 8080)
        self.manager.update_node_status(node_id, "new_status")
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.nodes[node_id].status, "new_status")

        node_id = self.manager.register_node(10.0, "test_role", "192.168.1.1", 8080)
        self.manager.nodes[node_id].last_heartbeat -= 40  # Simulate no heartbeat
        self.manager.monitor_nodes(failure_threshold=30)
        self.assertNotIn(node_id, self.manager.nodes)

        self.manager.register_node(20.0, "role2", "192.168.1.2", 8080)
        all_nodes = self.manager.get_all_nodes()
        self.assertEqual(len(all_nodes), 2)

system/c_backend/core/core_processing.c
C

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <complex.h>
#include "core_processing.h"

// Function to perform complex Fast Fourier Transform (FFT)
// This is a placeholder for a more optimized FFT implementation
void complex_fft(double complex *data, int n) {
    if (n <= 1) return;

    double complex even[n / 2];
    double complex odd[n / 2];
    for (int i = 0; i < n / 2; i++) {
        even[i] = data[2 * i];
        odd[i] = data[2 * i + 1];
    }

    complex_fft(even, n / 2);
    complex_fft(odd, n / 2);

    for (int k = 0; k < n / 2; k++) {
        double complex t = cexp(-2 * M_PI * I * k / n) * odd[k];
        data[k] = even[k] + t;
        data[k + n / 2] = even[k] - t;
    }
}

// Function to calculate the magnitude of a complex number
double complex_magnitude(double complex z) {
    return cabs(z);
}

// Function to calculate the phase of a complex number
double complex_phase(double complex z) {
    return carg(z);
}

// Function to perform element-wise addition of two complex arrays
void complex_array_add(double complex *arr1, double complex *arr2, double complex *result, int n) {
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        result[i] = arr1[i] + arr2[i];
    }
}

// Function to perform element-wise multiplication of two complex arrays
void complex_array_multiply(double complex *arr1, double complex *arr2, double complex *result, int n) {
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        result[i] = arr1[i] * arr2[i];
    }
}

// Function to normalize a complex array
void complex_array_normalize(double complex *arr, int n) {
    double norm = 0.0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        norm += complex_magnitude(arr[i]) * complex_magnitude(arr[i]);
    }
    norm = sqrt(norm);
    if (norm > 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            arr[i] /= norm;
        }
    }
}
